#### Synthesis Summary
15 material issue(s) require attention. Highest-priority issue: issue-001 (high) — Inventory authority and temporality must be closed in the ontology before it can serve as a reliable WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline. The current model lets live allocation, WMS/ERP reconciliation, and future real-time inventory extension depend on current or nightly stock numbers whose source authority, effective time, and allocation basis are not explicit. Controlled deliberation did not leave a blocking unresolved disagreement for the highest-priority issue.

- Highest severity: high

#### Material Issues
- issue-001 (high)
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for inventory allocation, WMS/ERP reconciliation, order receipt, and shipment decisions. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·재고 개념의 권위와 시간성 정합성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for inventory allocation and WMS/ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문 접수, 재고 할당, 출하 판단의 권위와 시간성을 안정적으로 정의하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, nightly reconciliations, multiple WMS/ERP sources, or historical inventory lookup depend on quantity_on_hand or nightly InventoryAggregate.available_qty as the allocation basis. Source finding context: 일중 주문, 부분 재고 변동, WMS/ERP 불일치 상황에서 allocation이 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty나 권위가 불명확한 quantity_on_hand를 기준으로 판단될 때. Source finding context: When intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, or nightly reconciliations occur, the ontology cannot reconstruct the inventory basis used for allocation or audit a later discrepancy. Source finding context: 야간 배치 기반 집계에서 일중·실시간 재고 이벤트, 복수 WMS/ERP 출처, 예약 재고 또는 과거 시점 조회를 추가하는 경우.
  - impact: The ontology cannot state which system and time basis a stock value represents, making allocation decisions hard to justify, WMS/ERP differences hard to audit, and real-time inventory evolution disruptive. Source finding context: 통합 기준 문서가 어느 시스템의 어느 시점 재고를 믿어야 하는지 닫지 못해 과할당, 품절 오판, 사후 회계 정합과 물리 재고 운영의 혼선을 유발할 수 있다. Source finding context: Allocation decisions can be impossible to justify after the fact, and WMS/ERP quantity differences cannot be traced through a shared conceptual model. Source finding context: 기존 available_qty/quantity_on_hand 값이 어떤 권위와 시점의 값인지 보존하지 못해 기존 데이터와 신규 데이터의 의미가 갈라지고, 통합 규칙을 기존 구조 수정 없이 확장하기 어렵다.

- issue-005 (medium)
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for SKU, freight, shipment, carrier rating, routing, and capacity integration. Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for SKU, freight, and carrier integration. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합에서 SKU, 출하, 운송 요율·제약 판단에 쓰이는 수량 단위의 공통 기준 제공.

  - impact: Shipment measurements can silently mix incompatible units, and historical shipment totals cannot be reliably interpreted, converted, or audited after unit standardization. Source finding context: The model can permit silent unit mixing in operational integration points such as cartonization, carrier rating, routing, and freight reconciliation. Source finding context: 기존 숫자 필드의 단위 의미가 보존되지 않아 신규 단위 체계와 기존 출하 데이터를 같은 기준으로 변환하거나 감사하기 어렵다.

- issue-008 (medium)
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for cargo movement, delivery routes, shipment status, and order fulfillment relations. Source finding context: TMS 통합에서 전체 화물, 배송 구간, 주문 충족 관계를 확장 가능한 개념 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: Concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, cargo, and delivery.
  - failure condition: Multi-leg, hub-transfer, multiple-carrier, leg-specific ETA/status, or reverse-logistics routes require DeliveryLeg instances that are not themselves warehouse-to-customer shipments. Source finding context: 복수 구간, 복수 운송사, 허브 환적, 구간별 상태/ETA 또는 반품 경로를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: When a delivery route contains warehouse-to-hub or hub-to-customer legs, those DeliveryLeg instances must both be Shipment instances and not satisfy the stated Shipment definition.
  - impact: Leg-level records can inherit whole-shipment status, carrier, weight, and fulfills relations, making classification inconsistent and route extension difficult without remodeling. Source finding context: 구간 엔티티가 전체 Shipment 의미를 상속해 신규 경로 유형을 기존 구조 수정 없이 추가하기 어렵고, 주문 충족 관계가 구간 단위로 잘못 확장될 위험이 있다. Source finding context: The ontology cannot consistently classify leg-level and shipment-level records, weakening integration rules that rely on inherited Shipment status, carrier, and fulfillment semantics.

  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for consistently distinguishing order, cargo, and delivery concepts. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 개념을 일관되게 구분하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Multi-leg delivery treats DeliveryLeg as a shipment, order fulfillment unit, or whole cargo movement. Source finding context: 다구간 배송에서 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment로 취급되어 구간 단위가 주문 충족 단위 또는 전체 출하 단위로 해석될 때.
  - impact: TMS leg status and OMS/ERP shipment fulfillment status can be mapped as if equivalent, weakening delivery completion, freight, and order fulfillment judgments. Source finding context: TMS의 leg 상태와 OMS/ERP의 shipment fulfillment 상태가 같은 개념처럼 매핑되어 배송 완료, 운임, 주문 충족 판단의 기준이 흔들린다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:40-50`, `materialized-input.md:52-58`, `materialized-input.md:105-109`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40,52,54,106`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - problem definition: DeliveryLeg-as-Shipment makes leg-level TMS state appear equivalent to whole-shipment fulfillment state.
  - problem framing: symptom / next_step_blocker / carry_forward / may_close_during_next_stage / observed

- issue-010 (medium)
  - affected purpose: Inventory concept authority and state clarity for WMS/ERP integration. Source finding context: 재고 개념의 권위와 상태를 WMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 명확히 하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Inventory lookup, allocation, or accounting adjustment interprets quantity_on_hand alternately as WMS physical stock or ERP accounting quantity. Source finding context: 재고 조회, 할당, 회계 조정에서 quantity_on_hand 하나를 WMS 실물 수량 또는 ERP 회계 수량으로 상황별 해석할 때.
  - impact: Availability decisions and accounting adjustments can conflict because one field represents incompatible inventory meanings. Source finding context: 같은 필드가 서로 다른 재고 의미를 대표하여 가용성 판단과 회계 수량 조정의 기준이 충돌한다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:76-84`, `materialized-input.md:115-116`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76,81,83,116`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - problem definition: quantity_on_hand collapses WMS physical on-hand stock and ERP accounting/book inventory into one meaning.
  - problem framing: symptom / next_step_blocker / carry_forward / may_close_during_next_stage / observed

- issue-011 (medium)
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for freight and delivery unit consistency. Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 단위 정합성을 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 제공하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Multiple warehouses or source systems contribute SKU measurements to one Shipment, or total_weight is used for freight, loading, or delivery restrictions. Source finding context: 여러 창고나 소스 시스템의 SKU가 한 Shipment에 포함되거나 total_weight가 운임/적재/배송 제한 판단에 사용될 때.
  - impact: The same numeric value can be interpreted in incompatible units, making shipment totals and dimension-based operational decisions semantically unreliable. Source finding context: 동일 숫자가 kg 또는 lb로 해석될 수 있어 중량 합계와 치수 기반 운영 판단이 의미적으로 신뢰 불가능해진다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:33-38`, `materialized-input.md:48`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33,37,38,48`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - problem definition: Weight, dimensions, and total_weight lack explicit unit meaning, calculation time, and conversion rules.
  - problem framing: symptom / next_step_blocker / carry_forward / may_close_during_next_stage / observed

- issue-012 (medium)
  - affected purpose: Order and inventory integration clarity for availability meaning and time basis. Source finding context: 주문·재고 통합에서 재고 가용성의 의미와 시간 기준을 명확히 하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Intraday order allocation uses nightly snapshot available_qty as if it were current available stock. Source finding context: 일중 주문 할당이 nightly snapshot available_qty를 현재 가용량으로 사용할 때.
  - impact: Allocation criteria become unclear, creating over-allocation or overly conservative under-allocation risk. Source finding context: 재고 할당 기준이 현재 상태인지 스냅샷인지 불분명해 과할당 또는 보수적 미할당 같은 운영 위험을 낳는다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004`, `materialized-input.md:86-90`, `materialized-input.md:111-112`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86,90,112`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - problem definition: available_qty is named and consumed as current availability while representing a nightly snapshot used for intraday allocation.
  - problem framing: symptom / next_step_blocker / carry_forward / may_close_during_next_stage / observed

- issue-015 (medium)
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for shipment and delivery integration. Source finding context: Using the ontology as a concept baseline for shipment and delivery integration.
  - failure condition: A shipment has multiple delivery legs or tracking events that must be localized to a route segment. Source finding context: When a shipment has multiple delivery legs, the model cannot reconstruct which legs belong to the shipment or the ordered route for that shipment.
  - impact: TMS/carrier integrations cannot reliably connect route segments, locations, and tracking events back to the end-to-end shipment path. Source finding context: TMS/carrier integrations cannot reliably connect route segments, locations, and tracking events back to the end-to-end shipment path using the ontology structure.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40-58`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:68`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:105-109`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40-58,105-109`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - problem definition: The ontology lacks an ordered composition relation from an end-to-end Shipment or Route to its DeliveryLeg parts.
  - problem framing: root_cause / next_step_blocker / fix_now / must_close_before_next_stage / observed

- issue-016 (medium)
  - affected purpose: WMS/ERP inventory concept baseline for allocation and reconciliation. Source finding context: Using the ontology as the inventory concept baseline for WMS/ERP allocation and reconciliation.
  - failure condition: Allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty or trace it to warehouse-level inventory records and source systems. Source finding context: When allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty, the model cannot trace the aggregate back to warehouse-level inventory records or their source systems.
  - impact: Consumers can read the allocation quantity but cannot verify, reconcile, or route disputes through the ontology structure. Source finding context: Consumers can read the allocation quantity but cannot verify, reconcile, or route disputes through the ontology structure, weakening trust in cross-system inventory integration.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:111-116`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90,111-116`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - problem definition: InventoryAggregate used for allocation lacks derivation from source InventoryRecord sets, authority, warehouse scope, snapshot metadata, and calculation basis.
  - problem framing: enabler / next_step_blocker / carry_forward / may_close_during_next_stage / observed

#### Synthesized Material Issue Explanations
- issue-001 (high): Inventory authority and temporality must be closed in the ontology before it can serve as a reliable WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline. The current model lets live allocation, WMS/ERP reconciliation, and future real-time inventory extension depend on current or nightly stock numbers whose source authority, effective time, and allocation basis are not explicit.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: logic
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 3/3 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution
  - remaining disagreement: 0/3 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution
  - issue statement: Inventory authority and temporality are not closed enough for allocation, reconciliation, and later real-time inventory extension. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / InventoryRecord, InventoryAggregate, inventory allocation rule, inventory truth note Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90,111-116; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 재고 권위와 시간성을 야간 ERP 정합 및 일별 스냅샷에 맞추는 결정이 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서라는 목적과 어긋난다. Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 즉 이 온톨로지가 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로 적절해야 하며 특히 권위와 시간성을 검토해야 한다는 기준이다. 대상은 여러 시스템의 재고 진실이 갈리는 지점을 통합 기준으로 닫지 않고, 운영상 빠르게 변하는 주문 할당을 야간 ERP 스냅샷으로 처리하도록 두어 목적상 중요한 재고 가용성 판단을 약화한다. Source finding context: 재고 개념에 system_of_record/source_authority, as_of/effective_at, snapshot_generated_at, reservation/allocation 이벤트를 추가하고, 일중 할당은 현재 가용 재고나 명시된 reservation ledger를 권위로 삼도록 기준을 분리한다. ERP 회계 수량 정합은 별도 reconciliation 상태와 시점으로 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·재고 개념의 권위와 시간성 정합성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 일중 주문, 부분 재고 변동, WMS/ERP 불일치 상황에서 allocation이 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty나 권위가 불명확한 quantity_on_hand를 기준으로 판단될 때. Source finding context: 통합 기준 문서가 어느 시스템의 어느 시점 재고를 믿어야 하는지 닫지 못해 과할당, 품절 오판, 사후 회계 정합과 물리 재고 운영의 혼선을 유발할 수 있다. Source finding context: 재고 권위와 시간성을 온톨로지의 필수 개념으로 닫지 않고 야간 ERP 정합 및 스냅샷 사용을 운영 규칙으로 수용한 설계 결정. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand는 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가지며 야간 배치로 ERP 기준에 맞춘다고 정의된다. Source finding context: InventoryRecord에는 as_of가 없어 주문 접수 당시 재고 같은 시점 질의가 불가능하다고 대상 자체가 명시한다. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 매일 밤 스냅샷인데, 일중 주문 재고 할당도 이 값을 읽도록 규칙화되어 있다. Source finding context: 이 조합은 사용자가 요구한 권위·시간성 중심의 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 목적을 약화한다. Source finding context: Inventory temporal and allocation coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:71-85, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:106-107 Source finding context: Inventory is modeled as current and nightly aggregate quantities without effective time, history, or reservation/allocation events. Source finding context: Inventory in fulfillment is time-dependent. Without temporal validity and allocation events, the model cannot reconstruct stock at order placement, explain oversell/short-pick conditions, or distinguish physical quantity, available-to-promise, reserved quantity, and accounting quantity. Source finding context: Introduce inventory event or snapshot concepts with `as_of`/effective interval, source system, quantity type, and reason; add reservation/allocation records that connect order lines to inventory at a specific time and location. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for inventory allocation and WMS/ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: When intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, or nightly reconciliations occur, the ontology cannot reconstruct the inventory basis used for allocation or audit a later discrepancy. Source finding context: Allocation decisions can be impossible to justify after the fact, and WMS/ERP quantity differences cannot be traced through a shared conceptual model. Source finding context: Inventory coverage stops at current quantity and daily aggregate values rather than modeling time-indexed inventory facts and allocation events. Source finding context: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` is described as a current value held independently by WMS and ERP. Source finding context: The ontology comment says there is no effective-time concept for reconstructing inventory at order receipt. Source finding context: `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` is a nightly snapshot, yet intraday allocation reads it. Source finding context: InventoryRecord / InventoryAggregate 재고 시간성 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:81, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:90, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:112, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:116 Source finding context: 일중/실시간 재고 확장 시 기존 재고 구조에 시간·출처·스냅샷 권위 필드 추가가 필요해 기존 데이터 연속성이 깨진다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 문제는 단순히 현재 as_of가 없다는 표면 결함이 아니라, 일중 주문 처리라는 현재 사용 경로가 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty에 직접 결합되어 있다는 점이다. 새 재고 소스나 더 빠른 동기화 주기를 추가하면 같은 available_qty 숫자가 어느 시점·어느 권위·어느 계산 기준의 값인지 구분할 수 없으므로, 과거 주문의 당시 재고 판단과 신규 실시간 판단을 같은 구조 안에서 함께 유지하기 어렵다. WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로 쓰일 경우 재고 할당의 변경 이력이 추적되지 않아 운영상 과할당/미할당 원인 분석과 단계적 마이그레이션이 약해진다. Source finding context: InventoryQuantity를 시간·권위·목적이 있는 개념으로 분리하고, 최소한 source_system, authority_basis, as_of/effective_at, snapshot_id 또는 calculation_version을 추가한다. InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 원천 수량이 아니라 특정 계산 시점의 파생 스냅샷으로 명시하고, 할당 규칙은 읽는 재고 뷰의 freshness/authority 요구를 참조하도록 바꾼다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문 접수, 재고 할당, 출하 판단의 권위와 시간성을 안정적으로 정의하는 목적. Source finding context: 야간 배치 기반 집계에서 일중·실시간 재고 이벤트, 복수 WMS/ERP 출처, 예약 재고 또는 과거 시점 조회를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 기존 available_qty/quantity_on_hand 값이 어떤 권위와 시점의 값인지 보존하지 못해 기존 데이터와 신규 데이터의 의미가 갈라지고, 통합 규칙을 기존 구조 수정 없이 확장하기 어렵다. Source finding context: 재고 수량 개념이 현재 숫자 값과 야간 집계 결과에 고정되어 있고, 시간·출처·계산 버전이 개념 모델의 일급 속성으로 분리되어 있지 않다. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand는 현재 보유 수량으로만 정의되고 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가진다고 설명된다. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 매일 밤 배치로 저장되는 스냅샷인데, 일중 주문 할당도 이 값을 읽도록 규칙이 고정되어 있다. Source finding context: WMS 실물 기준과 ERP 회계 기준의 불일치는 야간 배치 조정으로만 설명되어, 새 재고 출처나 실시간 동기화 주기를 기존 의미와 병행할 확장 지점이 없다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for inventory allocation, WMS/ERP reconciliation, order receipt, and shipment decisions. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·재고 개념의 권위와 시간성 정합성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for inventory allocation and WMS/ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문 접수, 재고 할당, 출하 판단의 권위와 시간성을 안정적으로 정의하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, nightly reconciliations, multiple WMS/ERP sources, or historical inventory lookup depend on quantity_on_hand or nightly InventoryAggregate.available_qty as the allocation basis. Source finding context: 일중 주문, 부분 재고 변동, WMS/ERP 불일치 상황에서 allocation이 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty나 권위가 불명확한 quantity_on_hand를 기준으로 판단될 때. Source finding context: When intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, or nightly reconciliations occur, the ontology cannot reconstruct the inventory basis used for allocation or audit a later discrepancy. Source finding context: 야간 배치 기반 집계에서 일중·실시간 재고 이벤트, 복수 WMS/ERP 출처, 예약 재고 또는 과거 시점 조회를 추가하는 경우.
  - impact: The ontology cannot state which system and time basis a stock value represents, making allocation decisions hard to justify, WMS/ERP differences hard to audit, and real-time inventory evolution disruptive. Source finding context: 통합 기준 문서가 어느 시스템의 어느 시점 재고를 믿어야 하는지 닫지 못해 과할당, 품절 오판, 사후 회계 정합과 물리 재고 운영의 혼선을 유발할 수 있다. Source finding context: Allocation decisions can be impossible to justify after the fact, and WMS/ERP quantity differences cannot be traced through a shared conceptual model. Source finding context: 기존 available_qty/quantity_on_hand 값이 어떤 권위와 시점의 값인지 보존하지 못해 기존 데이터와 신규 데이터의 의미가 갈라지고, 통합 규칙을 기존 구조 수정 없이 확장하기 어렵다.
  - root hypothesis: Inventory availability is modeled as current/nightly quantities without first-class authority, effective time, source qualification, or allocation/reservation events.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:81-84`, `materialized-input.md:86-90`, `materialized-input.md:111-116`,   - source lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose because inventory allocation, order receipt, shipment decisions, and WMS/ERP reconciliation all require knowing which inventory value is authoritative and when it applies. If intraday allocation reads a nightly InventoryAggregate.available_qty or an ambiguous InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand, the baseline cannot justify allocation decisions, reconstruct stock at order time, or audit later WMS/ERP discrepancies.
  - root cause: The starting point is that inventory availability is modeled as current/nightly quantities rather than as time- and authority-qualified inventory facts. Because source authority, effective/as-of time, source qualification, quantity purpose, and reservation/allocation events are not first-class concepts, every downstream use inherits the same ambiguity about what a quantity means and whether it is valid for live allocation, accounting reconciliation, or historical audit.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported across axiology, coverage, and evolution findings. InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand is treated as a current value that WMS and ERP may each hold, with mismatch resolved by nightly ERP adjustment; the model also states there is no effective-time concept for reconstructing inventory at order receipt. InventoryAggregate.available_qty is a nightly snapshot, yet intraday allocation reads it. Together, those choices make a nightly or authority-unclear quantity stand in for live operational availability, which prevents reliable allocation explanation, discrepancy tracing, and continuity when adding real-time inventory sources or reservation ledgers.
  - action: Separate inventory quantity concepts by authority, purpose, source system, effective/as-of time, snapshot or calculation basis, and allocation/reservation events. Treat nightly ERP reconciliation as its own reconciliation state and timestamped accounting alignment, not as the live allocation authority. Allocation rules should reference an explicitly authorized and fresh inventory view or reservation ledger, so physical availability, available-to-promise, reserved quantity, and accounting quantity can evolve without changing the meaning of existing records.

- issue-004 (high): Partial, split, substituted, backordered, or exception fulfillment cannot be represented at the order-line quantity grain because fulfillment is modeled only from Shipment to Order, not from shipment or allocation activity to specific OrderLine quantities.

  - issue statement: Partial, split, substituted, backordered, or line-level shipment fulfillment cannot be represented at the order-line quantity grain. Source finding context: OrderLine fulfillment coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:18, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104 Source finding context: OrderLine-level fulfillment mapping is absent, so partial shipments cannot be represented at the line level. Source finding context: For a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline, fulfillment coverage needs to answer which SKU quantity on which order line was picked, shipped, split, backordered, or delivered. An order-level `fulfills` relation collapses those distinctions and makes line-level reconciliation across OMS, WMS, TMS, and ERP unavailable. Source finding context: Add a line-level fulfillment/allocation concept such as `FulfillmentLine` or `ShipmentLine` with `order_line_ref`, `shipment_ref`, fulfilled quantity, shipped quantity, and status; keep the order-level relation as a derived aggregate if still needed. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, inventory allocation, shipment, and delivery. Source finding context: When an order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, or backorder, the ontology cannot state which order-line quantity moved in which shipment or leg. Source finding context: Operational reconciliation and exception handling become non-actionable because the integration model cannot tie physical movement and delivery evidence back to the commercial order line. Source finding context: The ontology only defines shipment-to-order fulfillment and omits a fulfillment concept at the order-line quantity grain. Source finding context: `Order` contains a list of `OrderLine` objects, but `OrderLine` only has SKU and quantity attributes. Source finding context: The only fulfillment relation is `Shipment -> Order`, explicitly at order grain rather than order-line grain. Source finding context: The target comment states that partial-shipment tracing from line to shipment is not possible in the ontology.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for order, inventory allocation, shipment, delivery, reconciliation, and exception handling. Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, inventory allocation, shipment, and delivery.
  - failure condition: An order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, backorder, or line-level delivery exception. Source finding context: When an order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, or backorder, the ontology cannot state which order-line quantity moved in which shipment or leg.
  - impact: The model cannot tie physical movement and delivery evidence back to the commercial order line, making reconciliation and exceptions non-actionable. Source finding context: Operational reconciliation and exception handling become non-actionable because the integration model cannot tie physical movement and delivery evidence back to the commercial order line.
  - root hypothesis: Fulfillment is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity, while OrderLine has no shipment, leg, or allocation fulfillment edge.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:18`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:18, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-004`, `finding-004.cause-001`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-24`, `finding-004.cause-002`, `finding-004.cause-003`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:25-26`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This materially weakens the WMS/TMS/ERP baseline because reconciliation and exception handling depend on knowing which SKU quantity on which commercial order line was picked, shipped, delivered, backordered, substituted, or cancelled. Order-level fulfillment collapses those distinctions, so physical movement and delivery evidence cannot be tied back to the obligations that operations and finance need to reconcile.
  - root cause: The root cause starts at the ontology grain: Order has OrderLine objects, but OrderLine carries only SKU and quantity attributes and has no fulfillment, shipment, leg, package, or allocation edge. Since the only fulfillment relation is Shipment -> Order, every downstream fulfillment fact is attached to the order aggregate rather than to the line quantity that was actually fulfilled or not fulfilled.
  - causal path: The causal path is direct: OrderLine exists as the commercial quantity unit, but it is not structurally connected to fulfillment execution; Shipment fulfills only Order; therefore a multi-line order with partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, backorder, or line-level delivery exception cannot state which line quantity moved in which shipment or leg. The deliberation accepted this coverage gap without further dispute: coverage raised it, other applicable lenses supported it, and logic was only not applicable because the issue is missing coverage rather than an inconsistency.
  - action: Add a line-level fulfillment or allocation concept, such as FulfillmentLine or ShipmentLine, that connects OrderLine to Shipment and, where needed, DeliveryLeg or package evidence. It should carry fulfilled quantity, shipped quantity, and fulfillment status so order-level fulfillment can remain only as a derived aggregate after the line-level truth exists.

Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / OrderLine-Shipment fulfillment structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:23,25-31,40-50,105-109 Source finding context: OrderLine is not structurally connected to the Shipment that fulfills it. Source finding context: The evidence explicitly states that OrderLine has no reference to the shipment/leg that fulfilled it, while the declared relation deliberately remains at order level. Because the ontology is meant to be a WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept standard, this missing edge breaks a core path between order detail and physical shipment detail, especially for partial shipments or split fulfillment. Source finding context: Add an explicit line-level fulfillment structure, such as ShipmentLine or FulfillmentAllocation, relating OrderLine to Shipment with fulfilled_qty and, if needed, DeliveryLeg/package references. Keep the order-level fulfills relation as a derived or summary relation if useful. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001 Source finding context: Using the ontology as the concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery integration across WMS/TMS/ERP. Source finding context: When one order has multiple lines, partial fulfillment, split shipments, or line-level exceptions, the model cannot identify which OrderLine quantity was fulfilled by which Shipment or route segment. Source finding context: Integration consumers cannot reconcile order-line fulfillment with shipment execution, shipment weight composition, delivery status, or exceptions from the target ontology itself. Source finding context: The fulfillment relation is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity, while OrderLine is left without a shipment/leg fulfillment edge. Source finding context: OrderLine only contains sku_ref and qty, with no attribute or relation to Shipment, DeliveryLeg, or a fulfillment allocation entity. Source finding context: The sole declared fulfillment relation is Shipment -> Order and its note says OrderLine-level mapping is not included. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight depends on 구성 OrderLine weights, so the target already assumes a structural connection that is not represented.

  - failure condition: An order has multiple lines, partial fulfillment, split shipments, or line-level exceptions. Source finding context: When one order has multiple lines, partial fulfillment, split shipments, or line-level exceptions, the model cannot identify which OrderLine quantity was fulfilled by which Shipment or route segment.
  - impact: Consumers cannot reconcile order-line fulfillment with shipment execution, shipment weight composition, delivery status, or exceptions from the ontology itself. Source finding context: Integration consumers cannot reconcile order-line fulfillment with shipment execution, shipment weight composition, delivery status, or exceptions from the target ontology itself.
  - root hypothesis: The fulfillment relation is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity while OrderLine lacks a shipment or leg fulfillment edge.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:23`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:25-31`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:105-109`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:23,25-31,40-50,105-109`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-019`, `finding-019.cause-001`, `finding-019.cause-002`, `finding-019.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-014`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_now, follow_up
  - materiality: This materially weakens the declared WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline because order-line fulfillment is a core reconciliation path between commercial order detail and shipment execution. Under multiple lines, partial fulfillment, split shipments, or line-level exceptions, consumers cannot determine line-level fulfillment status, shipment composition, delivery progress, or exception responsibility from the ontology itself.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts at the fulfillment grain: the modeled fulfillment relation exists only from Shipment to Order, while OrderLine has no relation or attribute connecting it to Shipment, DeliveryLeg, package, or a fulfillment allocation entity. That order-level-only structure prevents the model from carrying fulfillment facts at the line quantity level.
  - causal path: OrderLine contains only sku_ref and qty, so it cannot identify the shipment or leg that fulfilled any quantity. The sole fulfillment relation is Shipment -> Order and explicitly excludes OrderLine-level mapping. At the same time, Shipment.total_weight depends on composed OrderLine weights, so the ontology already assumes a line-to-shipment composition that it does not represent. Together, those steps produce the observed gap: line-level fulfillment cannot be reconciled to shipment execution.
  - action: Add an explicit line-level fulfillment structure, such as ShipmentLine or FulfillmentAllocation, relating OrderLine to Shipment with fulfilled_qty and optional DeliveryLeg or package references. Keep the existing order-level fulfills relation only as a derived or summary relation if useful, because the line-level allocation must be the authoritative structure for partial, split, and exception workflows.

- issue-002 (medium): issue-002는 소스 시스템별 중량·치수 단위 관례를 그대로 허용하는 설계가 물류 통합 기준 문서의 운영 위험 감소 목적을 약화한다는 material issue이다. 다만 독립 remediation root라기보다 issue-005/issue-011의 canonical measurement-unit cluster에 속하는 source-convention/value-alignment 증상으로 유지되어야 한다.

Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33-49; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 특히 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험 검토 요청이다. 총중량, 운임, 적재, 라벨, carrier handoff에 쓰일 수 있는 값을 단위 없는 number/string으로 합산 가능하게 두면 통합 기준이 운영 위험을 예방하기보다 각 연동의 관례 차이를 그대로 전파한다. Source finding context: weight와 dims를 수치 값과 canonical unit으로 분리하고, 원천 단위와 변환 시점/방법을 보존한다. Shipment.total_weight는 canonical unit 기반의 계산 결과와 계산 기준 시점을 함께 갖도록 하며, 단위 미확정 값은 출하 확정 계산에 사용할 수 없게 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험을 다루는 목적. Source finding context: 서로 다른 창고 또는 소스 시스템의 kg/lb, 치수 문자열이 Shipment.total_weight나 배송 연동 값으로 합산·전달될 때. Source finding context: 단위 불일치가 운임 산정, carrier 인계, 적재 계획, 배송 예외 처리의 오류로 이어질 수 있는데, 기준 문서가 이를 막는 공통 의미를 제공하지 못한다. Source finding context: 물류 수량 단위를 canonical concept로 승격하지 않고 source-system convention을 통합 기준 안에 그대로 허용한 설계 결정. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 number지만 kg 또는 lb를 창고별 관례대로 쓴다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이고 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 저장되므로 단위가 다른 원천 값이 합산 기준으로 들어갈 수 있다. Source finding context: 이 설계는 사용자가 요청한 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험 중심의 적합성 기준을 약화한다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험을 다루는 목적.
  - failure condition: 서로 다른 창고 또는 소스 시스템의 kg/lb, 치수 문자열이 Shipment.total_weight나 배송 연동 값으로 합산·전달될 때.
  - impact: 단위 불일치가 운임 산정, carrier 인계, 적재 계획, 배송 예외 처리의 오류로 이어질 수 있는데, 기준 문서가 이를 막는 공통 의미를 제공하지 못한다.
  - root hypothesis: 물류 수량 단위를 canonical concept로 승격하지 않고 source-system convention을 통합 기준 안에 그대로 허용한 설계 결정.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:37-38`, `materialized-input.md:48-49`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33-49; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-002`, `finding-002.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:37`, `finding-002.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:38`, `finding-002.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:48`, `finding-002.cause-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준은 주문·화물·배송 값이 운임 산정, 적재 계획, 라벨, carrier handoff, 배송 예외 처리에서 일관되게 해석되도록 해야 한다. 그런데 Sku.weight와 Sku.dims가 kg/lb 또는 문자열 단위를 소스 시스템 관례에 맡기고 Shipment.total_weight가 OrderLine 중량 합으로 저장되면, 서로 다른 단위 값이 합산·전달될 수 있다. 이는 통합 기준이 단위 불일치 위험을 예방하지 못하고 각 연동의 관례 차이를 운영 경로로 전파하게 만든다.
  - root cause: 문제의 시작점은 물류 수량 단위를 canonical concept로 승격하지 않고 source-system convention을 통합 모델 안에서 허용한 결정이다. upstream deliberation은 이를 더 넓은 measurement-continuity root로 좁혔으며, 측정값에는 canonical unit, 원천 단위, 변환 기준, 계산 timestamp/version, 단위 미확정 값의 계산 제외 규칙이 함께 있어야 한다고 정리했다.
  - causal path: causal path는 Sku.weight가 단위 없는 number로 남고, Sku.dims가 소스별 단위 문자열로 남으며, Shipment.total_weight가 구성 OrderLine 중량 합으로 저장되는 흐름이다. 이 조합은 단위가 다른 원천 값이 동일한 계산·배송 연동 값으로 들어갈 가능성을 만들고, 결과적으로 사용자가 요청한 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험 검토 기준을 약화한다.
  - action: weight와 dims를 수치 값과 canonical unit으로 분리하고 원천 단위와 변환 시점·방법을 보존해야 한다. Shipment.total_weight는 canonical unit 기반 계산 결과와 계산 기준 시점/version을 포함해야 하며, 단위가 확정되지 않은 값은 출하 확정 계산에 사용할 수 없도록 모델링해야 한다. remediation은 issue-005/issue-011의 measurement-unit cluster와 함께 처리해 중복 수정 없이 같은 root를 닫는 순서가 적절하다.

- issue-003 (medium): State interpretation is materially weak because order fulfillment, shipment, carrier tracking, and displayed ETA/state values are not governed by a canonical versioned status model; instead, meaning and authority are delegated to independent systems, integrations, and operations judgment.

Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type, status integration rule Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:19-22,44-49,60-68,111-117; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 상태 모델의 매핑과 최종 표시 권위를 각 연동·운영 판단에 맡기는 결정은 통합 기준 문서의 상태·권위 정합 목적에서 drift를 만든다. Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 즉 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서로서 상태 모델과 권위를 검토해야 한다는 기준이다. 대상은 상태 전이를 통합 의미로 정렬하지 않고 연동별 해석에 위임해, 동일 배송 사건이 주문 상태·화물 상태·추적 이벤트·ETA 표시에서 서로 다른 의미를 가질 수 있게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 상태 모델과 권위 정합성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 캐리어 이벤트, TMS shipment 상태, OMS 주문 상태, 운영팀 ETA 수정이 서로 다른 시점이나 의미를 가지며 통합 화면·후속 프로세스에서 함께 사용될 때. Source finding context: 상태와 ETA의 canonical authority 및 precedence를 온톨로지에서 정의하지 않고 각 시스템·연동·운영 판단에 분산시킨 설계 결정. Source finding context: Order.fulfillment_status는 OMS가 관리하고 Shipment.status는 별도 TMS 성격의 화물 상태로 정의된다. Source finding context: 무결성 규칙은 Order, Shipment, TrackingEvent 상태를 각 시스템이 독립 관리한다고 명시한다. Source finding context: ETA는 carrier API와 운영팀 수기 조정값이 섞이고 최종 표시값이 운영팀 판단에 따른다고 되어 있어 표시 권위와 원천 권위가 분리되지 않는다. Source finding context: 이 조합은 사용자가 요청한 상태 모델·권위 정합성 기준에 대해 통합 기준 문서의 목적 부합성을 낮춘다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type와 Shipment.status 상태 확장 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:44, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:63, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:65, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:66, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:113 Source finding context: 새 운송사 상태 코드나 외부 표준 변경을 수용하려면 enum과 연동별 매핑을 반복 수정해야 하며 상태 의미의 연속성이 보장되지 않는다. Source finding context: 변경 내성의 핵심은 외부 표준이 바뀌어도 기존 이벤트와 규칙의 의미를 보존하는 것이다. 현재 모델은 carrier-native 상태, canonical 상태, 매핑 버전, 적용 기간을 분리하지 않아 상태 변화가 발생하면 enum 확장과 개별 연동 로직 수정이 동시에 필요하다. 그 결과 과거 TrackingEvent가 당시 어떤 매핑 규칙으로 Shipment.status에 영향을 줬는지 재현하기 어렵고, WMS/TMS/ERP 간 상태 통합 규칙도 연동별 구현으로 흩어진다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent에는 carrier_code, native_status_code, native_status_version을 보존하고, 별도 StatusMapping 개념으로 canonical Shipment.status, mapping_version, effective_from/to, precedence를 모델링한다. enum은 내부 canonical 상태로 한정하고 외부 코드는 데이터로 확장 가능하게 둔다.
