  - impact: Past and current states may be interpreted by different integration rules, weakening reproducibility and causing drift in delivered, delayed, exception, or completed decisions. Source finding context: 연동별 상태 해석이 분산되면 배송 완료, 예외, 지연, 주문 완료 판단의 기준이 달라져 통합 문서가 운영 의사결정의 기준 역할을 하지 못한다. Source finding context: 상태 enum 또는 각 연동 구현을 직접 바꿔야 하며, 과거 이벤트가 어떤 매핑 기준으로 해석됐는지 온톨로지 수준에서 유지되지 않는다.
  - root hypothesis: Carrier-native statuses, canonical shipment/order statuses, mapping precedence, and mapping versions are not modeled as first-class concepts.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:19-22`, `materialized-input.md:44-49`, `materialized-input.md:63-67`, `materialized-input.md:111-117`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:19-22,44-49,60-68,111-117; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:44`, `materialized-input.md:46`, `materialized-input.md:63`, `materialized-input.md:65`, `materialized-input.md:66`, `materialized-input.md:113`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:44, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:63, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:65, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:66, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:113`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-003`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-011`, `rel-010`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-010`, `finding-003.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:44-47`, `finding-003.cause-002`, `finding-003.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:111-113`, `finding-003.cause-004`, `materialized-input.md:49`, `materialized-input.md:117`, `finding-003.cause-005`, `finding-011.cause-001`, `finding-011.cause-002`, `finding-011.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-005`, `rel-011`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-006`, `rel-012`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline purpose because the ontology is supposed to provide a common state and authority reference for order, shipment, tracking, and carrier coordination. When carrier events, TMS shipment statuses, OMS order statuses, ETA overrides, or new carrier status codes are used together, delivered, delayed, exception, and completed decisions can drift by integration or time period rather than following one reproducible baseline.
  - root cause: The starting point is that carrier-native statuses, canonical shipment/order statuses, mapping precedence, and mapping versions are not first-class modeled concepts. Without those concepts, the ontology has no stable authority for preserving external status inputs, converting them into internal meanings, deciding conflicts or freshness, or reconstructing which mapping rule applied historically.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported by two converging findings: axiology shows that Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type, and ETA display authority are independently managed or left to integrations and operations judgment; evolution shows that fixed enums and integration-specific mappings cannot preserve meaning across new carriers, changed status codes, or historical replay. Together, those steps explain why the same shipping event can produce inconsistent current or historical meanings across OMS, TMS, carrier tracking, ERP-facing processes, and integrated screens.
  - action: Add a canonical fulfillment/shipment state model and separate it from native carrier status data. Preserve carrier_code, native_status_code, and native_status_version; introduce versioned StatusMapping with canonical status, mapping_version, effective dates, precedence, conflict resolution, freshness rules, and displayed-state authority. ETA overrides should be modeled separately from carrier ETA with audit and display-decision rules, so source truth, operational override, and final presentation do not collapse into one field.

- issue-005 (medium): SKU weight, SKU dimensions, and Shipment.total_weight need canonical measurement semantics because the current model leaves physical quantities as source-local numbers or strings that cannot be trusted across warehouse, carrier, or unit-rule changes.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: logic, structure
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 2/2 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: coverage, evolution
  - remaining disagreement: 0/2 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: coverage, evolution
  - issue statement: SKU and shipment measurement quantities lack canonical unit semantics and are brittle under warehouse or carrier unit changes. Source finding context: Quantity and measurement unit coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:28-33, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:35-44 Source finding context: SKU dimensions and weights lack canonical units, while shipment weight is derived from them. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration depends on consistent physical quantities for rating, routing, capacity, labels, and customs-adjacent operations even if international shipment is out of scope. Deriving shipment weight from unitless or source-local measurements creates a coverage gap in the unit dimension of the ontology. Source finding context: Add measurement value objects or attributes for numeric value plus canonical unit, dimensional components, conversion basis, and source unit; require derived shipment totals to specify unit and derivation timestamp. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for SKU, freight, and carrier integration. Source finding context: When different warehouses or systems provide kg/lb or dimension strings with different conventions, shipment totals and carrier-facing measurements cannot be interpreted reliably from the ontology. Source finding context: The model can permit silent unit mixing in operational integration points such as cartonization, carrier rating, routing, and freight reconciliation. Source finding context: Measurement units are not modeled as first-class concepts or required attributes for physical quantities. Source finding context: SKU weight is a number whose unit follows each warehouse's local convention. Source finding context: SKU dimensions are a free-form string whose units vary by source system. Source finding context: Shipment total weight is calculated and stored from order-line weights, inheriting the missing-unit problem. Source finding context: Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight 단위 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:37, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:38, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48 Source finding context: 창고·운송사별 단위 확장 시 기존 중량/치수 값의 의미가 변환 불가능해져 출하 중량 데이터의 연속성이 끊긴다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 단위는 나중에 붙일 표시 정보가 아니라 수량의 의미를 구성하는 권위 정보다. 현재 구조는 단위가 값과 분리되어 있지 않아, lb 창고와 kg 창고를 동시에 운영하거나 운송사가 부피중량/실중량 규칙을 요구하는 변경이 오면 기존 숫자들을 해석·변환·검증할 기준이 없다. 특히 total_weight가 출하 확정 시 저장되는 파생값이므로, 단위 보존 없이 계산된 과거 shipment 데이터는 단위 표준화 이후에도 신뢰 가능한 비교 기준으로 유지되기 어렵다. Source finding context: 중량과 치수를 Quantity 값 객체로 모델링해 value, unit, measurement_system/source, measured_at 또는 calculation_basis를 포함한다. Shipment.total_weight도 구성 SKU 중량의 단순 숫자 합이 아니라 단위 변환 규칙과 계산 버전을 가진 파생 측정값으로 정의한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합에서 SKU, 출하, 운송 요율·제약 판단에 쓰이는 수량 단위의 공통 기준 제공. Source finding context: kg/lb가 혼재된 창고, 다른 치수 단위를 쓰는 소스 시스템, 또는 단위 민감 운송사/요율 규칙을 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 기존 숫자 필드의 단위 의미가 보존되지 않아 신규 단위 체계와 기존 출하 데이터를 같은 기준으로 변환하거나 감사하기 어렵다. Source finding context: 측정값이 단위와 계산 기준을 포함한 독립 개념이 아니라 number/string 필드에 소스 관례로 저장된다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 숫자이며 창고별 계량 관례인 kg 또는 lb를 그대로 쓴다고 정의된다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이고 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다고 정의된다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 저장되지만 단위 또는 계산 기준 필드가 없다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for SKU, freight, shipment, carrier rating, routing, and capacity integration. Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for SKU, freight, and carrier integration. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합에서 SKU, 출하, 운송 요율·제약 판단에 쓰이는 수량 단위의 공통 기준 제공.
  - failure condition: Different warehouses, source systems, carriers, or rate rules introduce kg/lb or dimension-convention differences while Shipment.total_weight is calculated from unitless SKU values. Source finding context: When different warehouses or systems provide kg/lb or dimension strings with different conventions, shipment totals and carrier-facing measurements cannot be interpreted reliably from the ontology. Source finding context: kg/lb가 혼재된 창고, 다른 치수 단위를 쓰는 소스 시스템, 또는 단위 민감 운송사/요율 규칙을 추가하는 경우.
  - impact: Shipment measurements can silently mix incompatible units, and historical shipment totals cannot be reliably interpreted, converted, or audited after unit standardization. Source finding context: The model can permit silent unit mixing in operational integration points such as cartonization, carrier rating, routing, and freight reconciliation. Source finding context: 기존 숫자 필드의 단위 의미가 보존되지 않아 신규 단위 체계와 기존 출하 데이터를 같은 기준으로 변환하거나 감사하기 어렵다.
  - root hypothesis: Physical measurements are stored as source-local number/string fields without unit, source, conversion basis, or calculation version.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:28-33`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:35-44`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:28-33, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:35-44`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:37`, `materialized-input.md:38`, `materialized-input.md:48`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:37, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:38, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-006`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-010`, `rel-008`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-008`, `finding-006.cause-001`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:28-32`, `finding-006.cause-002`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:33`, `finding-006.cause-003`, `finding-010.cause-001`, `finding-010.cause-002`, `finding-010.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-005`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared WMS/TMS/ERP baseline purpose because freight rating, routing, capacity, cartonization, and reconciliation depend on comparable physical quantities. If warehouses or source systems mix kg/lb or dimension conventions, shipment totals derived from unitless SKU values can silently combine incompatible units, and historical totals cannot be reliably interpreted, converted, or audited after standardization.
  - root cause: The starting point is that physical measurements are modeled as raw number/string fields without unit, source or measurement system, conversion basis, measured/calculated time, or calculation version. Because the unit semantics are absent at the measurement source, every downstream derived value inherits ambiguity before carrier or shipment logic can safely use it.
  - causal path: The accepted causal path is: Sku.weight is a numeric value tied to each warehouse's local convention; Sku.dims is a free-form string whose unit convention varies by source system; Shipment.total_weight is then calculated and stored from those order-line weights without its own unit or calculation basis. That makes the shipment measurement appear authoritative while preserving none of the information needed to interpret or compare it.
  - action: Model weight and dimensions as quantity value objects containing value, canonical unit, source or measurement system, measured/calculated timestamp, conversion basis, and source unit where relevant. Derived shipment totals should also be modeled as derived measurement values with unit, derivation timestamp, conversion rule, and calculation version, so unit normalization is established before shipment, carrier-rating, routing, capacity, and audit workflows depend on the values.

- issue-006 (medium): Manual ETA overrides and inventory reconciliation changes are a material auditability gap: the ontology can hold changed ETA or inventory values, but it cannot represent who changed them, when, on what basis, or what prior and new values were involved.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: logic
  - insufficient evidence lenses: structure
  - resolution accepted by: 1/1 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: coverage
  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: coverage
  - issue statement: Manual ETA overrides and inventory reconciliation changes lack audit evidence for actor, time, basis, and prior/new values. Source finding context: Manual and reconciliation audit evidence coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:44, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:76-79, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:110-112 Source finding context: Manual ETA overrides and inventory reconciliation adjustments lack audit evidence concepts. Source finding context: The coverage lens asks whether controlled actions have evidence for actor, time, and basis. Manual ETA changes and inventory reconciliation directly affect customer communication and stock truth, so a concept baseline that omits audit evidence cannot support accountability or dispute analysis. Source finding context: Add audit/control entities such as `ManualOverride`, `InventoryAdjustment`, or `OperationalDecision` with actor, occurred_at, source value, prior value, new value, reason, approval/reference evidence, and affected entity references. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: Use as an operationally trustworthy WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline. Source finding context: When an ETA or inventory value changes due to manual judgment or batch reconciliation, the ontology cannot represent who changed it, when, from what evidence, or why. Source finding context: Operational risk increases because customer-facing and accounting/warehouse reconciliation changes cannot be audited or challenged from the shared model. Source finding context: The ontology represents final mutable values but omits control-action evidence concepts for manual and batch changes. Source finding context: Shipment ETA can be adjusted manually by the field operations team. Source finding context: The final displayed ETA mixes carrier API values with manual operation-team judgment. Source finding context: Inventory quantities are held separately by WMS and ERP and adjusted through nightly reconciliation, but no adjustment evidence concept is modeled.
  - affected purpose: Operationally trustworthy WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for customer-facing ETA and accounting/warehouse inventory reconciliation changes. Source finding context: Use as an operationally trustworthy WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline.
  - failure condition: ETA or inventory values change due to manual operations judgment or nightly reconciliation. Source finding context: When an ETA or inventory value changes due to manual judgment or batch reconciliation, the ontology cannot represent who changed it, when, from what evidence, or why.
  - impact: Customer-facing and stock-truth changes cannot be audited, challenged, or explained from the shared ontology. Source finding context: Operational risk increases because customer-facing and accounting/warehouse reconciliation changes cannot be audited or challenged from the shared model.
  - root hypothesis: The ontology represents final mutable values but omits control-action evidence concepts for manual and batch changes.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:44`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:76-79`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:110-112`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:44, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:76-79, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:110-112`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-007`, `finding-007.cause-001`, `finding-007.cause-002`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:112`, `finding-007.cause-003`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:110-111`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-006`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-004`, `rel-006`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-006`, `rel-012`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose of an operationally trustworthy WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline because customer-facing ETA changes and accounting/warehouse stock-truth changes must be explainable and challengeable. Without audit evidence for manual judgment and reconciliation adjustments, the shared model cannot support accountability, dispute analysis, or operational trust when mutable values change.
  - root cause: The starting point is that the ontology represents final mutable values while omitting first-class control-action evidence concepts for manual and batch changes. Because the model lacks entities for overrides, operational decisions, or inventory adjustments, later states cannot carry actor, time, basis, prior value, new value, reason, approval/reference evidence, and affected-entity links.
  - causal path: The causal path shows that shipment ETA can be manually adjusted by field operations, and the displayed ETA mixes carrier API values with operations-team judgment. It also shows that inventory quantities are maintained separately by WMS and ERP and adjusted through nightly reconciliation. In both paths, the ontology captures the resulting value but not the control action that changed it, so the model loses the evidence needed to audit or explain the change.
  - action: Add audit/control concepts for manual overrides, operational decisions, and inventory adjustments before the next stage. These concepts should link each controlled change to actor, occurred_at, source or basis value, prior value, new value, reason, approval or reference evidence, and affected shipment or inventory entities, so ETA and inventory-truth changes become traceable rather than only final-state values.

- issue-007 (medium): Common non-happy-path and post-delivery fulfillment outcomes cannot currently be represented as order or shipment lifecycle states. The lifecycle model should be fixed before the next stage because it otherwise forces materially different outcomes into happy-path states or external handling.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: logic, structure
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 1/1 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: coverage
  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: coverage
  - issue statement: Common non-happy-path and post-delivery outcomes cannot be represented as order or shipment lifecycle states. Source finding context: Order and shipment lifecycle coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:14-17, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:39-42, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:55-63 Source finding context: The lifecycle states cover only the happy path and omit cancellation, failure, return, correction, and post-delivery exception handling. Source finding context: Fulfillment operations require non-happy-path and post-terminal coverage: cancellations, failed delivery, loss/damage, return-to-sender, returns, redelivery, proof-of-delivery correction, and reopened exceptions. Without those categories, the concept model cannot express common operational outcomes or distinguish final completion from later correction. Source finding context: Extend lifecycle models with explicit non-happy-path and post-delivery states/events, such as canceled, delivery_failed, exception_open, exception_resolved, return_initiated, returned, corrected/reissued, and define how tracking events transition or annotate shipment/order state. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-005 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for order, shipment, and delivery state models. Source finding context: When an order or shipment is canceled, fails delivery, returns, receives a carrier exception, or needs post-delivery correction, the ontology has no lifecycle category to represent the outcome without overloading `delivered` or external handling. Source finding context: Integrations can diverge on state interpretation and cannot consistently model operational risk events that materially affect customer service, inventory, and settlement. Source finding context: Lifecycle coverage is limited to forward fulfillment states and does not include exception resolution or terminal correction concepts. Source finding context: Order fulfillment status progresses from received to delivered with no cancellation, return, failure, or correction states. Source finding context: Shipment status progresses from created to delivered and notes that delivered is final. Source finding context: Tracking events can report `exception`, but no shipment/order lifecycle state or resolution concept corresponds to that event.
  - affected purpose: Concept baseline for order, shipment, delivery state models, customer service, inventory, and settlement. Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for order, shipment, and delivery state models.
  - failure condition: An order or shipment is canceled, fails delivery, returns, receives a carrier exception, or requires post-delivery correction. Source finding context: When an order or shipment is canceled, fails delivery, returns, receives a carrier exception, or needs post-delivery correction, the ontology has no lifecycle category to represent the outcome without overloading `delivered` or external handling.
  - impact: Integrations can diverge on operational-risk state interpretation and may overload delivered or external handling for materially different outcomes. Source finding context: Integrations can diverge on state interpretation and cannot consistently model operational risk events that materially affect customer service, inventory, and settlement.
  - root hypothesis: Lifecycle coverage is limited to forward happy-path fulfillment states without exception, return, failure, cancellation, resolution, or correction concepts.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-005`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:14-17`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:39-42`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:55-63`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:14-17, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:39-42, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:55-63`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-008`, `finding-008.cause-001`, `finding-008.cause-002`, `finding-008.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-007`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-005`, `rel-011`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: The declared purpose is to provide a concept baseline for order, shipment, and delivery state models that can support customer service, inventory, and settlement. A baseline that cannot represent cancellation, delivery failure, returns, carrier exceptions, or post-delivery corrections leaves integrations without shared operational-risk meanings, so different systems may interpret or overload states inconsistently.
  - root cause: The root cause is that lifecycle coverage is limited to forward happy-path fulfillment states and lacks exception, return, failure, cancellation, resolution, and correction concepts. This is the starting point because the missing state categories prevent later order, shipment, and tracking-event behavior from being modeled consistently.
  - causal path: The causal path shows order fulfillment progressing from received to delivered without cancellation, return, failure, or correction states; shipment status likewise progresses from created to delivered and treats delivered as final. Tracking events can report an exception, but there is no corresponding shipment or order lifecycle state or resolution concept. Together, those gaps mean common exception and post-terminal outcomes cannot be represented without overloading delivered or moving the meaning outside the ontology.
  - action: Extend the lifecycle models with explicit non-happy-path and post-delivery states or events, such as canceled, delivery_failed, exception_open, exception_resolved, return_initiated, returned, corrected, or reissued. Also define how tracking events transition or annotate shipment and order state, because the tracking exception concept must connect to the canonical lifecycle rather than remain an isolated event value.

- issue-008 (medium): DeliveryLeg should not remain a subtype of Shipment. The current model makes a route segment inherit the meaning of an end-to-end warehouse-to-customer shipment, so leg-level records are classified and related as whole shipments even when they only represent one segment of a route.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: coverage
  - resolution accepted by: 2/2 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: evolution, logic
  - remaining disagreement: 0/2 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: evolution, logic
  - issue statement: DeliveryLeg inherits Shipment meaning even though route segments and end-to-end shipments have different classification, lifecycle, and fulfillment scopes. Source finding context: Shipment와 DeliveryLeg 상속 구조 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:41, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:52, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:54, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:105, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:106, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:107, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:108 Source finding context: 다구간·환적·역물류 확장 시 배송 구간이 전체 화물의 속성과 주문 충족 관계를 상속해 경로 모델을 수정해야 한다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 전체 Shipment와 구간 DeliveryLeg는 lifecycle, 상태, 운송사, ETA, 주문 충족 책임이 다르게 진화하는 축이다. 현재는 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment를 상속하므로 구간이 전체 shipment의 fulfills 관계와 total_weight 의미에 끌려 들어간다. 다구간 TMS 통합이 들어오면 구간별 carrier/status/ETA는 필요하지만 구간이 주문 전체를 충족한다고 볼 수 없는 경우가 생겨 기존 구조가 확장보다 재분류를 요구한다. Source finding context: Shipment를 전체 consignment/fulfillment movement로 두고 DeliveryLeg는 상속이 아니라 route component 관계로 연결한다. ShipmentRoute 또는 LegAssignment를 추가해 leg_seq, from/to, carrier, leg_status, leg_eta를 구간 속성으로 분리하고, fulfills는 전체 Shipment 또는 별도 FulfillmentAllocation에만 두도록 범위를 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004 Source finding context: TMS 통합에서 전체 화물, 배송 구간, 주문 충족 관계를 확장 가능한 개념 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 복수 구간, 복수 운송사, 허브 환적, 구간별 상태/ETA 또는 반품 경로를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 구간 엔티티가 전체 Shipment 의미를 상속해 신규 경로 유형을 기존 구조 수정 없이 추가하기 어렵고, 주문 충족 관계가 구간 단위로 잘못 확장될 위험이 있다. Source finding context: 전체 화물 이동과 경로 구간이 상속 관계로 결합되어 각자의 독립적인 lifecycle과 관계 범위가 분리되지 않는다. Source finding context: Shipment는 창고에서 고객으로 가는 물리적 화물 이동으로 정의되고 DeliveryLeg도 Shipment의 일종이라고 설명된다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 is_a: Shipment로 선언되어 전체 Shipment의 status, total_weight, eta, carrier_ref 구조를 상속한다. Source finding context: fulfills 관계는 Shipment에서 Order로 정의되어 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment 하위형인 한 구간에도 주문 충족 관계가 적용될 수 있다. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment hierarchy Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:35-49 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is declared as a Shipment even though its own definition allows segments that do not satisfy the Shipment definition. Source finding context: The conflict is inter-claim: the superclass definition at lines 35-36 imposes a warehouse-to-customer movement shape, and the subclass definition at lines 47-49 allows partial route segments. This makes at least the example leg instances unsatisfiable as Shipment instances without weakening either the Shipment definition or the subtype assertion. For a WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline, this also propagates Shipment-level status, carrier, and fulfillment relations onto leg-level objects whose lifecycle is not necessarily equivalent to an end-to-end shipment. Source finding context: Separate end-to-end `Shipment` from `DeliveryLeg` composition, for example model `Shipment` has many `DeliveryLeg`s instead of `DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment`; or broaden `Shipment` to a generic transport movement and introduce a distinct end-to-end shipment concept for warehouse-to-customer fulfillment. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, cargo, and delivery. Source finding context: When a delivery route contains warehouse-to-hub or hub-to-customer legs, those DeliveryLeg instances must both be Shipment instances and not satisfy the stated Shipment definition. Source finding context: The ontology cannot consistently classify leg-level and shipment-level records, weakening integration rules that rely on inherited Shipment status, carrier, and fulfillment semantics. Source finding context: The model uses subtype inheritance for a part-of route segment relationship. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is declared with `is_a: Shipment`. Source finding context: `Shipment` is defined as an end-to-end warehouse-to-customer movement. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is defined as a route segment and explicitly includes warehouse-to-hub and hub-to-customer examples, which are partial movements rather than each being warehouse-to-customer movements.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for cargo movement, delivery routes, shipment status, and order fulfillment relations. Source finding context: TMS 통합에서 전체 화물, 배송 구간, 주문 충족 관계를 확장 가능한 개념 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: Concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, cargo, and delivery.
  - failure condition: Multi-leg, hub-transfer, multiple-carrier, leg-specific ETA/status, or reverse-logistics routes require DeliveryLeg instances that are not themselves warehouse-to-customer shipments. Source finding context: 복수 구간, 복수 운송사, 허브 환적, 구간별 상태/ETA 또는 반품 경로를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: When a delivery route contains warehouse-to-hub or hub-to-customer legs, those DeliveryLeg instances must both be Shipment instances and not satisfy the stated Shipment definition.
  - impact: Leg-level records can inherit whole-shipment status, carrier, weight, and fulfills relations, making classification inconsistent and route extension difficult without remodeling. Source finding context: 구간 엔티티가 전체 Shipment 의미를 상속해 신규 경로 유형을 기존 구조 수정 없이 추가하기 어렵고, 주문 충족 관계가 구간 단위로 잘못 확장될 위험이 있다. Source finding context: The ontology cannot consistently classify leg-level and shipment-level records, weakening integration rules that rely on inherited Shipment status, carrier, and fulfillment semantics.
  - root hypothesis: A route segment is modeled with subtype inheritance from end-to-end Shipment instead of composition, conflating leg lifecycle and fulfillment semantics with whole-shipment semantics.

`.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: evolution, logic
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline because multi-leg, hub-transfer, multiple-carrier, leg-specific ETA/status, and reverse-logistics routes need DeliveryLeg records that are not themselves complete shipments. With the current inheritance, integration rules can incorrectly apply whole-shipment status, carrier, weight, and order-fulfillment relations to individual route legs.
  - root cause: The starting point is the modeling choice to represent a part-of route segment with subtype inheritance: DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment. Because Shipment is the end-to-end cargo movement concept, that subtype assertion imports whole-shipment lifecycle and fulfillment semantics into a leg concept whose lifecycle, carrier assignment, ETA, and responsibility can differ from the parent shipment.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported by both evolution and logic findings: Shipment is defined as the warehouse-to-customer movement; DeliveryLeg is declared as a Shipment while also being defined as a route segment, including warehouse-to-hub and hub-to-customer examples; and Shipment-to-Order fulfillment relations can therefore reach DeliveryLeg through inheritance. This makes partial legs simultaneously behave as Shipment instances and fail the stated Shipment definition in common multi-leg routes.
  - action: Separate end-to-end Shipment from DeliveryLeg composition. Model Shipment as owning or being connected to ordered route legs through a ShipmentRoute or LegAssignment structure, with leg_seq, from/to, carrier, leg_status, and leg_eta on the leg or assignment. Keep fulfills on the whole Shipment, or move allocation semantics to a separate FulfillmentAllocation if fulfillment must be split explicitly.

- issue-009 (medium): DeliveryLeg must not remain modeled as a subtype of Shipment. In the reviewed ontology, that inheritance makes a route segment look like the same kind of thing as the whole shipment, so leg-level TMS state can be mistaken for whole-shipment fulfillment state.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, semantics, structure, evolution, logic
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: coverage

  - accepted lenses: semantics, coverage, evolution, logic, axiology, structure
  - remaining disagreement: 0/6 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: semantics
  - issue statement: DeliveryLeg as a Shipment makes leg-level TMS states and whole-shipment fulfillment semantics appear to be the same concept. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg.is_a Shipment Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40,52,54,106 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 하위유형으로 둔 것은 구간과 전체 화물 이동의 의미를 혼동한다. Source finding context: 구간은 전체 출하/화물의 구성 요소이지 동일한 존재론적 유형이 아니다. 특히 허브행 구간은 고객에게 가는 전체 Shipment가 아니며, Shipment.status의 delivered 최종 상태와 DeliveryLeg의 구간 도착 완료 상태도 같은 의미가 아니다. WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서에서 이 상속은 출하 단위 추적, 구간 상태, 주문 충족 여부의 의미를 섞어 시스템 간 매핑 오류를 만들 수 있다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 하위유형이 아니라 ShipmentRoute 또는 ShipmentLeg 같은 구성 요소로 모델링하고, Shipment가 여러 DeliveryLeg를 갖는 관계를 둔다. 구간 상태와 전체 Shipment 상태를 별도 enum으로 분리하고, fulfills 관계는 전체 Shipment 또는 실제 주문라인 충족 단위에만 붙인다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 개념을 일관되게 구분하는 목적. Source finding context: 다구간 배송에서 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment로 취급되어 구간 단위가 주문 충족 단위 또는 전체 출하 단위로 해석될 때. Source finding context: TMS의 leg 상태와 OMS/ERP의 shipment fulfillment 상태가 같은 개념처럼 매핑되어 배송 완료, 운임, 주문 충족 판단의 기준이 흔들린다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg의 존재론적 유형을 Shipment의 하위유형으로 선언했다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 경로의 한 구간으로 정의되어 전체 창고-고객 이동인 Shipment와 다른 의미를 가진다. Source finding context: 상속 때문에 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment의 속성과 fulfills(Order) 관계까지 받는 완전한 Shipment처럼 해석된다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for consistently distinguishing order, cargo, and delivery concepts. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 개념을 일관되게 구분하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Multi-leg delivery treats DeliveryLeg as a shipment, order fulfillment unit, or whole cargo movement. Source finding context: 다구간 배송에서 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment로 취급되어 구간 단위가 주문 충족 단위 또는 전체 출하 단위로 해석될 때.
  - impact: TMS leg status and OMS/ERP shipment fulfillment status can be mapped as if equivalent, weakening delivery completion, freight, and order fulfillment judgments. Source finding context: TMS의 leg 상태와 OMS/ERP의 shipment fulfillment 상태가 같은 개념처럼 매핑되어 배송 완료, 운임, 주문 충족 판단의 기준이 흔들린다.
  - root hypothesis: DeliveryLeg is declared as a Shipment, so route segment meaning is confused with whole cargo movement meaning.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:40-50`, `materialized-input.md:52-58`, `materialized-input.md:105-109`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40,52,54,106`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-014`, `finding-014.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:40-41`, `materialized-input.md:52-54`, `finding-014.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:43-50`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-009`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.logic`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: The declared purpose is to provide a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline that consistently distinguishes order, cargo, and delivery concepts. This issue weakens that purpose because multi-leg delivery depends on separating a leg's operational progress from the whole shipment's customer-facing and fulfillment meaning; if they are mapped as equivalent, delivery completion, freight handling, and order fulfillment judgments can be made at the wrong grain.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with DeliveryLeg being declared as a Shipment. That type declaration gives a route component the identity and inherited semantics of a complete cargo movement, including shipment-level status and fulfillment relationships, before any downstream mapping or integration rule can distinguish the two.
  - causal path: DeliveryLeg is defined as one segment of a route, while Shipment represents the whole warehouse-to-customer cargo movement. Because DeliveryLeg inherits from Shipment, a hub-bound or intermediate leg can be interpreted as a full shipment and can inherit Shipment.status and fulfills(Order) semantics. That makes a leg arrival or leg completion state appear comparable to delivered or fulfilled at the whole-shipment level, which is the observed semantic confusion.
  - action: Model DeliveryLeg as a route component, such as part of a ShipmentRoute or ShipmentLeg structure, rather than as a Shipment subtype. Then connect Shipment to one or more DeliveryLeg instances through composition, split leg status from whole Shipment status, and keep fulfills constrained to the whole shipment or the actual fulfillment allocation grain. This should be handled with the broader DeliveryLeg inheritance-versus-composition correction so the semantic fix and structural type fix land together.

- issue-010 (medium): quantity_on_hand should be retained as a material, narrowed issue: it hides the difference between WMS physical on-hand stock and ERP accounting/book inventory, so the ontology cannot state which inventory quantity is authoritative for a given decision.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, evolution, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: logic
  - insufficient evidence lenses: coverage
  - resolution accepted by: 5/5 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: semantics, coverage, structure, axiology, evolution
  - remaining disagreement: 0/5 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: semantics
  - issue statement: quantity_on_hand collapses physical and accounting inventory meanings into one attribute. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76,81,83,116 Source finding context: quantity_on_hand가 실물 재고와 회계 재고라는 서로 다른 의미를 한 속성명에 합친다. Source finding context: '현재 보유 수량'이라는 단일 이름은 물리적으로 창고에 있는 수량과 회계/ERP 기준 수량을 같은 의미로 보이게 만든다. 두 값은 권위, 사용 목적, 조정 시점이 다르므로 같은 속성으로 두면 통합 기준 문서가 어떤 값을 재고 진실로 삼는지 모호해진다. Source finding context: quantity_on_hand를 physical_on_hand_qty(WMS authority)와 accounting_inventory_qty 또는 erp_book_qty(ERP authority)처럼 의미와 권위를 분리한다. 각 속성에 source_system, authority, as_of/reconciled_at을 붙이고 조정 결과는 별도 reconciliation 개념으로 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: 재고 개념의 권위와 상태를 WMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 명확히 하는 목적. Source finding context: 재고 조회, 할당, 회계 조정에서 quantity_on_hand 하나를 WMS 실물 수량 또는 ERP 회계 수량으로 상황별 해석할 때. Source finding context: 같은 필드가 서로 다른 재고 의미를 대표하여 가용성 판단과 회계 수량 조정의 기준이 충돌한다. Source finding context: 서로 다른 권위와 의미를 가진 WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고를 quantity_on_hand라는 단일 속성으로 표현했다. Source finding context: quantity_on_hand는 현재 보유 수량으로 정의된다. Source finding context: 동일 설명 안에서 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가지며 기준도 실물과 회계로 다르다고 밝힌다.
  - affected purpose: Inventory concept authority and state clarity for WMS/ERP integration. Source finding context: 재고 개념의 권위와 상태를 WMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 명확히 하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Inventory lookup, allocation, or accounting adjustment interprets quantity_on_hand alternately as WMS physical stock or ERP accounting quantity. Source finding context: 재고 조회, 할당, 회계 조정에서 quantity_on_hand 하나를 WMS 실물 수량 또는 ERP 회계 수량으로 상황별 해석할 때.
  - impact: Availability decisions and accounting adjustments can conflict because one field represents incompatible inventory meanings. Source finding context: 같은 필드가 서로 다른 재고 의미를 대표하여 가용성 판단과 회계 수량 조정의 기준이 충돌한다.
  - root hypothesis: WMS physical inventory and ERP accounting inventory are represented by one quantity_on_hand property despite different authority, purpose, and reconciliation timing.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:76-84`, `materialized-input.md:115-116`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76,81,83,116`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-015`, `finding-015.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:81-83`, `finding-015.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:83`, `materialized-input.md:116`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-010`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-001`, `rel-003`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose of clarifying inventory authority and state for WMS/ERP integration. If lookup, allocation, or accounting adjustment reads the same quantity_on_hand field as either physical stock or book inventory depending on context, availability decisions and accounting corrections can conflict while appearing to use one shared inventory truth.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts where two inventory quantities with different authorities, purposes, source systems, timing, and reconciliation needs are modeled as one property. WMS physical inventory and ERP accounting/book inventory are not merely two labels for the same value; they answer different questions and become valid at different points in the operational and reconciliation flow.
  - causal path: The evidence path first shows quantity_on_hand defined as the current held quantity, then shows the same description assigning values to both WMS and ERP with physical versus accounting bases. That makes the single property carry incompatible meanings, which creates the observed ambiguity about whether the field represents warehouse availability or ERP book quantity.
  - action: Split the concept into explicit quantities such as physical_on_hand_qty for WMS authority and accounting_inventory_qty or erp_book_qty for ERP authority. Each quantity should carry source_system, authority, and as_of or reconciled_at timing, while reconciliation or derivation between them should be represented separately. This should be handled as a concrete follow-up under the broader issue-001 inventory authority and temporality cluster, not as a replacement for that broader root issue.

- issue-011 (medium): Material issue: weight, dimensions, and shipment total_weight are not semantically reliable because their units, calculation basis, and conversion rules are not explicit. Deliberation narrowed this issue into issue-005's canonical measurement quantity scope while preserving issue-011's semantic requirements.
  - issue stance agreement: 3/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, evolution, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: logic, structure
  - insufficient evidence lenses: coverage
  - resolution accepted by: 4/4 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: semantics, coverage, axiology, evolution
  - remaining disagreement: 0/4 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: semantics
  - issue statement: Weight and dimension fields can mean kg, lb, or source-specific dimension systems under the same names. Source finding context: Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33,37,38,48 Source finding context: 중량·치수 속성이 단위를 의미의 일부로 고정하지 않아 같은 이름이 kg/lb와 소스별 치수 체계를 동시에 뜻한다. Source finding context: weight와 dims는 수치만으로 의미가 완성되지 않고 단위가 포함되어야 한다. 단위 없는 'weight'와 문자열 'dims'는 시스템별로 다른 의미를 같은 속성명 아래에 숨기며, total_weight 같은 파생값은 서로 다른 단위가 합산될 위험이 있다. Source finding context: weight_value와 weight_uom, dimension_value 구조(length/width/height)와 dimension_uom을 분리하거나 표준 단위로 정규화한다. total_weight에는 계산 기준 단위와 계산 시점, 원천 SKU/OrderLine 단위 변환 규칙을 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 단위 정합성을 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 여러 창고나 소스 시스템의 SKU가 한 Shipment에 포함되거나 total_weight가 운임/적재/배송 제한 판단에 사용될 때. Source finding context: 동일 숫자가 kg 또는 lb로 해석될 수 있어 중량 합계와 치수 기반 운영 판단이 의미적으로 신뢰 불가능해진다. Source finding context: 측정값 속성의 의미를 구성하는 단위를 별도 권위 있는 속성이나 표준 단위로 모델링하지 않았다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 kg 또는 lb를 창고별 관례대로 사용한다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이며 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 원천 중량들의 합으로 저장되어 단위 혼합이 파생값 의미까지 전파된다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for freight and delivery unit consistency. Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 단위 정합성을 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 제공하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Multiple warehouses or source systems contribute SKU measurements to one Shipment, or total_weight is used for freight, loading, or delivery restrictions. Source finding context: 여러 창고나 소스 시스템의 SKU가 한 Shipment에 포함되거나 total_weight가 운임/적재/배송 제한 판단에 사용될 때.
  - impact: The same numeric value can be interpreted in incompatible units, making shipment totals and dimension-based operational decisions semantically unreliable. Source finding context: 동일 숫자가 kg 또는 lb로 해석될 수 있어 중량 합계와 치수 기반 운영 판단이 의미적으로 신뢰 불가능해진다.
  - root hypothesis: Measurement units are not modeled as part of the semantic meaning of weight, dimensions, and derived shipment totals.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:33-38`, `materialized-input.md:48`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33,37,38,48`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-016`, `finding-016.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:37`, `finding-016.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:38`, `finding-016.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-011`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the WMS/TMS/ERP freight and delivery unit-consistency purpose because the same numeric field can mean kg, lb, or a source-specific dimension convention. When multiple warehouses or systems contribute SKU measurements to one shipment, freight, loading, and delivery restriction decisions can be based on incompatible quantities that look identical in the model.
  - root cause: The starting point is that measurement units are not modeled as part of the meaning of weight, dimensions, or derived shipment totals. Without an authoritative unit or canonical normalized quantity, downstream totals and operational interpretations inherit source conventions rather than a stable shared meaning.
  - causal path: Sku.weight can be interpreted by warehouse convention as kg or lb, and Sku.dims is a string whose dimension system varies by source. Shipment.total_weight is then stored as a sum of source weights, so any mixed-unit ambiguity propagates from SKU measurements into the derived shipment value used for operational decisions.
  - action: Consolidate the fix under issue-005's canonical measurement quantity work: represent weight and dimensions as explicit value-plus-unit fields or normalized quantities, and make total_weight carry its calculation unit, calculation time, conversion rules, and conversion history. This should be addressed before relying on shipment totals for freight, loading, or delivery constraints because the derived value depends on correct source-unit interpretation.

- issue-012 (medium): InventoryAggregate.available_qty should be carried forward as a material issue because it is named and consumed like current available inventory while representing a nightly snapshot used for intraday allocation.

  - issue statement: The available_qty name and use obscure whether allocation reads current availability or a stale snapshot. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86,90,112 Source finding context: available_qty라는 이름이 현재 가용 재고를 뜻하지만 실제로는 야간 스냅샷 값이다. Source finding context: 'available'은 주문 시점의 할당 가능 수량처럼 읽히지만, 모델의 설명은 전일 또는 야간 배치 시점의 스냅샷에 가깝다. 이름과 시간성이 어긋나면 OMS/WMS/ERP가 같은 값을 현재 가용 재고로 오해할 수 있다. Source finding context: available_qty_snapshot, snapshot_as_of 같은 시간성 있는 이름과 속성으로 바꾸거나, 현재 할당 가능 수량은 실시간 계산 개념으로 분리한다. 일중 할당 규칙은 어떤 시점의 재고를 권위로 삼는지 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004 Source finding context: 주문·재고 통합에서 재고 가용성의 의미와 시간 기준을 명확히 하는 목적. Source finding context: 일중 주문 할당이 nightly snapshot available_qty를 현재 가용량으로 사용할 때. Source finding context: 재고 할당 기준이 현재 상태인지 스냅샷인지 불분명해 과할당 또는 보수적 미할당 같은 운영 위험을 낳는다. Source finding context: 시간성 있는 스냅샷 값을 현재성을 암시하는 available_qty 이름으로 모델링했다. Source finding context: available_qty는 매일 밤 배치로 저장되는 스냅샷으로 설명된다. Source finding context: 동시에 일중 주문 할당도 이 값을 읽는다고 규칙화되어 현재 가용량처럼 사용된다.
  - affected purpose: Order and inventory integration clarity for availability meaning and time basis. Source finding context: 주문·재고 통합에서 재고 가용성의 의미와 시간 기준을 명확히 하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Intraday order allocation uses nightly snapshot available_qty as if it were current available stock. Source finding context: 일중 주문 할당이 nightly snapshot available_qty를 현재 가용량으로 사용할 때.
  - impact: Allocation criteria become unclear, creating over-allocation or overly conservative under-allocation risk. Source finding context: 재고 할당 기준이 현재 상태인지 스냅샷인지 불분명해 과할당 또는 보수적 미할당 같은 운영 위험을 낳는다.
  - root hypothesis: InventoryAggregate.available_qty is named as current availability while actually representing a nightly snapshot also used for intraday allocation.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004`, `materialized-input.md:86-90`, `materialized-input.md:111-112`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86,90,112`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-017`, `finding-017.cause-001`, `finding-017.cause-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-012`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-002`, `rel-004`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the purpose of clarifying availability meaning and time basis across order and inventory integration. If OMS/WMS/ERP consumers read available_qty as current allocatable stock when it is only a stale snapshot, allocation criteria become ambiguous and can cause either over-allocation or overly conservative under-allocation.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts at the model boundary where available_qty is given a current-availability name while its authority is a nightly snapshot. That naming and authority mismatch is upstream of the allocation behavior because consumers cannot tell whether they are reading live availability, snapshot availability, or a derived allocation quantity.
  - causal path: The evidence path is that available_qty is described as a value stored by a nightly batch, and intraday order allocation is also specified to read that same value. That combination makes a snapshot field operate as if it were current availability during allocation, which creates the observed stale-snapshot/currentness ambiguity.
  - action: Rename and model the value as a timestamped snapshot, such as available_qty_snapshot with snapshot_as_of and source traceability, or split current allocatable quantity into a separate real-time derived concept with explicit authority and freshness. This should be coordinated with issue-001's broader inventory temporality work so the available_qty-specific ambiguity is fixed without duplicating generic inventory authority remediation.

- issue-015 (medium): Issue-015 is a material route-composition gap: the ontology cannot reconstruct which DeliveryLeg records compose an end-to-end Shipment, or their order, for multi-leg delivery paths.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, evolution, semantics, structure, logic
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: coverage
  - resolution accepted by: 6/6 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: structure, coverage, logic, axiology, evolution, semantics
  - remaining disagreement: 0/6 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: structure
  - issue statement: The ontology cannot reconstruct the route legs belonging to a shipment. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Shipment-DeliveryLeg route structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40-58,105-109 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is typed as a Shipment but no structure connects an end-to-end Shipment to its legs. Source finding context: An is_a link says a DeliveryLeg is a kind of Shipment; it does not connect a multi-leg shipment to the ordered sequence of legs that compose its route. For a delivery concept model, this leaves the physical delivery path disconnected from the shipment it is supposed to detail. Source finding context: Introduce an explicit composition/sequence relation, for example Shipment has_many DeliveryLeg ordered by leg_seq, or a Route entity containing DeliveryLeg instances, and define whether TrackingEvent attaches to the whole Shipment, a leg, or both. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002 Source finding context: Using the ontology as a concept baseline for shipment and delivery integration. Source finding context: When a shipment has multiple delivery legs, the model cannot reconstruct which legs belong to the shipment or the ordered route for that shipment. Source finding context: TMS/carrier integrations cannot reliably connect route segments, locations, and tracking events back to the end-to-end shipment path using the ontology structure. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is modeled only through inheritance from Shipment, without a composition relation from a shipment or route to its legs. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg has leg_seq, from_ref, and to_ref, indicating a route segment, but no parent shipment or route reference. Source finding context: The only structural connection involving Shipment in the relations section is fulfills from Shipment to Order. Source finding context: TrackingEvent points to Shipment generally, so without a shipment-leg composition path events cannot be structurally localized to a route segment when needed.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for shipment and delivery integration. Source finding context: Using the ontology as a concept baseline for shipment and delivery integration.
  - failure condition: A shipment has multiple delivery legs or tracking events that must be localized to a route segment. Source finding context: When a shipment has multiple delivery legs, the model cannot reconstruct which legs belong to the shipment or the ordered route for that shipment.
  - impact: TMS/carrier integrations cannot reliably connect route segments, locations, and tracking events back to the end-to-end shipment path. Source finding context: TMS/carrier integrations cannot reliably connect route segments, locations, and tracking events back to the end-to-end shipment path using the ontology structure.
  - root hypothesis: DeliveryLeg is modeled through inheritance but no composition relation connects an end-to-end shipment or route to its ordered legs.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40-58`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:68`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:105-109`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40-58,105-109`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-020`, `finding-020.cause-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:52-58`, `finding-020.cause-002`, `finding-020.cause-003`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:60-68`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-015`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.logic`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-007`, `rel-016`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the WMS/TMS/ERP shipment and delivery integration baseline because integrations need a dependable structure for connecting route segments, locations, carrier responsibility, and tracking events back to the shipment path. When a shipment has multiple legs, the current model cannot identify the ordered route or localize events to a specific segment.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with DeliveryLeg being modeled through inheritance from Shipment instead of through an explicit composition relation. A subtype assertion can say what kind of thing a DeliveryLeg is, but it cannot state that a particular shipment or route owns an ordered set of legs.
  - causal path: DeliveryLeg carries route-segment properties such as leg_seq, from_ref, and to_ref, but lacks a parent shipment or route reference. The relations section only connects Shipment to Order through fulfills, leaving no shipment-to-leg path. TrackingEvent points generally to Shipment, so without ordered shipment/route-to-leg composition, tracking events cannot be structurally placed on the relevant route segment when that detail is needed.
  - action: Fix this before the next stage by adding an explicit ordered composition structure: either Shipment has_many DeliveryLeg ordered by leg_seq, or a Route entity contains ordered DeliveryLeg instances. Coordinate the fix with issue-008 because both share the inheritance-versus-composition cause, but preserve issue-015 as the distinct requirement for ordered route reconstruction and leg-level traceability. Also define whether TrackingEvent attaches to Shipment, DeliveryLeg, or both.

- issue-016 (medium): InventoryAggregate is a material structural traceability issue: allocation reads available_qty from an aggregate SKU quantity, but the ontology does not show which warehouse-scoped InventoryRecord records, source authorities, snapshot/as-of metadata, or calculation basis produced that value.

  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, evolution, semantics, structure

  - insufficient evidence lenses: coverage

  - remaining disagreement: 0/5 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none

  - issue statement: Allocation reads an aggregate inventory number whose source records and derivation cannot be traced. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / InventoryAggregate source structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90,111-116 Source finding context: InventoryAggregate is not structurally connected to the InventoryRecord records it aggregates. Source finding context: The ontology declares InventoryAggregate as the quantity used for allocation, including intraday orders, and notes that WMS and ERP each hold inventory truth that is reconciled by nightly batch. Without an explicit aggregate-from/source relation, the inventory path from warehouse-level records to enterprise availability is structurally incomplete. Source finding context: Add an explicit derivation or aggregation relation from InventoryAggregate to its source InventoryRecord set, including scope such as warehouse set, source system/authority, and snapshot/as_of metadata if the aggregate is batch-produced. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003 Source finding context: Using the ontology as the inventory concept baseline for WMS/ERP allocation and reconciliation. Source finding context: When allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty, the model cannot trace the aggregate back to warehouse-level inventory records or their source systems. Source finding context: Consumers can read the allocation quantity but cannot verify, reconcile, or route disputes through the ontology structure, weakening trust in cross-system inventory integration. Source finding context: The aggregate inventory concept is represented as a standalone SKU quantity without a structural derivation path from warehouse inventory records or inventory authorities. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate has only sku_ref and available_qty; it has no reference to InventoryRecord, Warehouse, WMS, ERP, or a source snapshot. Source finding context: InventoryRecord is the warehouse/SKU inventory record, but no relation connects it to InventoryAggregate. Source finding context: The allocation rule relies on InventoryAggregate.available_qty, making the disconnected aggregate a live structural dependency for order fulfillment.
  - affected purpose: WMS/ERP inventory concept baseline for allocation and reconciliation. Source finding context: Using the ontology as the inventory concept baseline for WMS/ERP allocation and reconciliation.
  - failure condition: Allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty or trace it to warehouse-level inventory records and source systems. Source finding context: When allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty, the model cannot trace the aggregate back to warehouse-level inventory records or their source systems.
  - impact: Consumers can read the allocation quantity but cannot verify, reconcile, or route disputes through the ontology structure. Source finding context: Consumers can read the allocation quantity but cannot verify, reconcile, or route disputes through the ontology structure, weakening trust in cross-system inventory integration.
  - root hypothesis: InventoryAggregate is represented as a standalone SKU quantity without structural derivation from warehouse inventory records, source systems, authorities, or snapshot metadata.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:111-116`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90,111-116`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-021`, `finding-021.cause-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86-90`, `finding-021.cause-002`, `finding-021.cause-003`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:111-112`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-016.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-016`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-016.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-016.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-016.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-016.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-016.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-003`, `rel-005`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the WMS/ERP inventory concept baseline for allocation and reconciliation because consumers can use the aggregate allocation quantity but cannot explain, verify, reconcile, or route disputes about available_qty back to warehouse records or source systems.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with InventoryAggregate being modeled as a standalone allocation-facing quantity. Because it carries only the aggregate SKU availability and lacks derivation links to InventoryRecord sets, warehouse scope, source authority, snapshot time, and calculation basis, every downstream allocation explanation inherits that missing provenance.
  - causal path: The structure finding shows that InventoryAggregate has sku_ref and available_qty but no reference to InventoryRecord, Warehouse, WMS, ERP, or a source snapshot. InventoryRecord is the warehouse/SKU record concept, yet no relation connects those records to the aggregate. Since the allocation rule relies on InventoryAggregate.available_qty, the disconnected aggregate becomes a live fulfillment dependency whose source cannot be traced when allocation or reconciliation needs an explanation.
  - action: Add explicit derivation or aggregation relations from InventoryAggregate to the source InventoryRecord sets, including warehouse scope, source authority, snapshot/as-of metadata, and calculation basis. This should be carried forward as the narrow derivation and traceability facet linked to the broader inventory authority issue, so available_qty can be audited without duplicating the wider issue-001 concern.

- issue-001: fix_now

This is a root modeling defect because downstream allocation, reconciliation, audit, and real-time extension all depend on knowing which inventory value is authoritative and when it applies. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / InventoryRecord, InventoryAggregate, inventory allocation rule, inventory truth note Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90,111-116; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 재고 권위와 시간성을 야간 ERP 정합 및 일별 스냅샷에 맞추는 결정이 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서라는 목적과 어긋난다. Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 즉 이 온톨로지가 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로 적절해야 하며 특히 권위와 시간성을 검토해야 한다는 기준이다. 대상은 여러 시스템의 재고 진실이 갈리는 지점을 통합 기준으로 닫지 않고, 운영상 빠르게 변하는 주문 할당을 야간 ERP 스냅샷으로 처리하도록 두어 목적상 중요한 재고 가용성 판단을 약화한다. Source finding context: 재고 개념에 system_of_record/source_authority, as_of/effective_at, snapshot_generated_at, reservation/allocation 이벤트를 추가하고, 일중 할당은 현재 가용 재고나 명시된 reservation ledger를 권위로 삼도록 기준을 분리한다. ERP 회계 수량 정합은 별도 reconciliation 상태와 시점으로 모델링한다. Source finding context: 일중 주문, 부분 재고 변동, WMS/ERP 불일치 상황에서 allocation이 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty나 권위가 불명확한 quantity_on_hand를 기준으로 판단될 때. Source finding context: 통합 기준 문서가 어느 시스템의 어느 시점 재고를 믿어야 하는지 닫지 못해 과할당, 품절 오판, 사후 회계 정합과 물리 재고 운영의 혼선을 유발할 수 있다.
