Source finding context: InventoryRecord에는 as_of가 없어 주문 접수 당시 재고 같은 시점 질의가 불가능하다고 대상 자체가 명시한다. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 매일 밤 스냅샷인데, 일중 주문 재고 할당도 이 값을 읽도록 규칙화되어 있다. Source finding context: 이 조합은 사용자가 요구한 권위·시간성 중심의 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 목적을 약화한다. Source finding context: Inventory temporal and allocation coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:71-85, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:106-107 Source finding context: Inventory is modeled as current and nightly aggregate quantities without effective time, history, or reservation/allocation events. Source finding context: Inventory in fulfillment is time-dependent. Without temporal validity and allocation events, the model cannot reconstruct stock at order placement, explain oversell/short-pick conditions, or distinguish physical quantity, available-to-promise, reserved quantity, and accounting quantity. Source finding context: Introduce inventory event or snapshot concepts with `as_of`/effective interval, source system, quantity type, and reason; add reservation/allocation records that connect order lines to inventory at a specific time and location. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for inventory allocation and WMS/ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: When intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, or nightly reconciliations occur, the ontology cannot reconstruct the inventory basis used for allocation or audit a later discrepancy. Source finding context: Allocation decisions can be impossible to justify after the fact, and WMS/ERP quantity differences cannot be traced through a shared conceptual model. Source finding context: Inventory coverage stops at current quantity and daily aggregate values rather than modeling time-indexed inventory facts and allocation events. Source finding context: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` is described as a current value held independently by WMS and ERP. Source finding context: The ontology comment says there is no effective-time concept for reconstructing inventory at order receipt. Source finding context: `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` is a nightly snapshot, yet intraday allocation reads it. Source finding context: InventoryRecord / InventoryAggregate 재고 시간성 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:81, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:90, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:112, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:116 Source finding context: 일중/실시간 재고 확장 시 기존 재고 구조에 시간·출처·스냅샷 권위 필드 추가가 필요해 기존 데이터 연속성이 깨진다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 문제는 단순히 현재 as_of가 없다는 표면 결함이 아니라, 일중 주문 처리라는 현재 사용 경로가 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty에 직접 결합되어 있다는 점이다. 새 재고 소스나 더 빠른 동기화 주기를 추가하면 같은 available_qty 숫자가 어느 시점·어느 권위·어느 계산 기준의 값인지 구분할 수 없으므로, 과거 주문의 당시 재고 판단과 신규 실시간 판단을 같은 구조 안에서 함께 유지하기 어렵다. WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로 쓰일 경우 재고 할당의 변경 이력이 추적되지 않아 운영상 과할당/미할당 원인 분석과 단계적 마이그레이션이 약해진다. Source finding context: InventoryQuantity를 시간·권위·목적이 있는 개념으로 분리하고, 최소한 source_system, authority_basis, as_of/effective_at, snapshot_id 또는 calculation_version을 추가한다. InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 원천 수량이 아니라 특정 계산 시점의 파생 스냅샷으로 명시하고, 할당 규칙은 읽는 재고 뷰의 freshness/authority 요구를 참조하도록 바꾼다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문 접수, 재고 할당, 출하 판단의 권위와 시간성을 안정적으로 정의하는 목적. Source finding context: 야간 배치 기반 집계에서 일중·실시간 재고 이벤트, 복수 WMS/ERP 출처, 예약 재고 또는 과거 시점 조회를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 기존 available_qty/quantity_on_hand 값이 어떤 권위와 시점의 값인지 보존하지 못해 기존 데이터와 신규 데이터의 의미가 갈라지고, 통합 규칙을 기존 구조 수정 없이 확장하기 어렵다. Source finding context: 재고 수량 개념이 현재 숫자 값과 야간 집계 결과에 고정되어 있고, 시간·출처·계산 버전이 개념 모델의 일급 속성으로 분리되어 있지 않다. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand는 현재 보유 수량으로만 정의되고 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가진다고 설명된다. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 매일 밤 배치로 저장되는 스냅샷인데, 일중 주문 할당도 이 값을 읽도록 규칙이 고정되어 있다. Source finding context: WMS 실물 기준과 ERP 회계 기준의 불일치는 야간 배치 조정으로만 설명되어, 새 재고 출처나 실시간 동기화 주기를 기존 의미와 병행할 확장 지점이 없다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-004: fix_now
  - rationale: The missing OrderLine-to-fulfillment grain prevents the declared baseline from tying physical movement and delivery evidence to commercial obligations. Multiple lenses support the issue, and it directly blocks common fulfillment scenarios. Source finding context: OrderLine fulfillment coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:18, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104 Source finding context: OrderLine-level fulfillment mapping is absent, so partial shipments cannot be represented at the line level. Source finding context: For a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline, fulfillment coverage needs to answer which SKU quantity on which order line was picked, shipped, split, backordered, or delivered. An order-level `fulfills` relation collapses those distinctions and makes line-level reconciliation across OMS, WMS, TMS, and ERP unavailable. Source finding context: Add a line-level fulfillment/allocation concept such as `FulfillmentLine` or `ShipmentLine` with `order_line_ref`, `shipment_ref`, fulfilled quantity, shipped quantity, and status; keep the order-level relation as a derived aggregate if still needed. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, inventory allocation, shipment, and delivery. Source finding context: When an order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, or backorder, the ontology cannot state which order-line quantity moved in which shipment or leg. Source finding context: Operational reconciliation and exception handling become non-actionable because the integration model cannot tie physical movement and delivery evidence back to the commercial order line. Source finding context: The ontology only defines shipment-to-order fulfillment and omits a fulfillment concept at the order-line quantity grain. Source finding context: `Order` contains a list of `OrderLine` objects, but `OrderLine` only has SKU and quantity attributes. Source finding context: The only fulfillment relation is `Shipment -> Order`, explicitly at order grain rather than order-line grain. Source finding context: The target comment states that partial-shipment tracing from line to shipment is not possible in the ontology.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-014: fix_now, follow_up
  - rationale: The structure lens directly supports the issue, but the projection also notes overlap with issue-004. It is classified as the structural expression of the same line-level fulfillment gap while still requiring closure in the target because the missing edge blocks reconciliation. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / OrderLine-Shipment fulfillment structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:23,25-31,40-50,105-109 Source finding context: OrderLine is not structurally connected to the Shipment that fulfills it. Source finding context: The evidence explicitly states that OrderLine has no reference to the shipment/leg that fulfilled it, while the declared relation deliberately remains at order level. Because the ontology is meant to be a WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept standard, this missing edge breaks a core path between order detail and physical shipment detail, especially for partial shipments or split fulfillment. Source finding context: Add an explicit line-level fulfillment structure, such as ShipmentLine or FulfillmentAllocation, relating OrderLine to Shipment with fulfilled_qty and, if needed, DeliveryLeg/package references. Keep the order-level fulfills relation as a derived or summary relation if useful. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001 Source finding context: Using the ontology as the concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery integration across WMS/TMS/ERP. Source finding context: When one order has multiple lines, partial fulfillment, split shipments, or line-level exceptions, the model cannot identify which OrderLine quantity was fulfilled by which Shipment or route segment. Source finding context: Integration consumers cannot reconcile order-line fulfillment with shipment execution, shipment weight composition, delivery status, or exceptions from the target ontology itself. Source finding context: The fulfillment relation is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity, while OrderLine is left without a shipment/leg fulfillment edge. Source finding context: OrderLine only contains sku_ref and qty, with no attribute or relation to Shipment, DeliveryLeg, or a fulfillment allocation entity. Source finding context: The sole declared fulfillment relation is Shipment -> Order and its note says OrderLine-level mapping is not included. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight depends on 구성 OrderLine weights, so the target already assumes a structural connection that is not represented.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-002: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: Deliberation narrowed this issue into the measurement-unit cluster represented by issue-005/issue-011. It remains a real operational-risk symptom of missing canonical units and conversion provenance rather than a separate remediation root. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33-49; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 중량과 치수 단위를 소스 시스템 관례에 맡기는 설계가 화물·배송 운영 위험을 줄이는 통합 기준 문서의 목적과 맞지 않는다. Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 특히 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험 검토 요청이다. 총중량, 운임, 적재, 라벨, carrier handoff에 쓰일 수 있는 값을 단위 없는 number/string으로 합산 가능하게 두면 통합 기준이 운영 위험을 예방하기보다 각 연동의 관례 차이를 그대로 전파한다. Source finding context: weight와 dims를 수치 값과 canonical unit으로 분리하고, 원천 단위와 변환 시점/방법을 보존한다. Shipment.total_weight는 canonical unit 기반의 계산 결과와 계산 기준 시점을 함께 갖도록 하며, 단위 미확정 값은 출하 확정 계산에 사용할 수 없게 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험을 다루는 목적. Source finding context: 서로 다른 창고 또는 소스 시스템의 kg/lb, 치수 문자열이 Shipment.total_weight나 배송 연동 값으로 합산·전달될 때. Source finding context: 단위 불일치가 운임 산정, carrier 인계, 적재 계획, 배송 예외 처리의 오류로 이어질 수 있는데, 기준 문서가 이를 막는 공통 의미를 제공하지 못한다. Source finding context: 물류 수량 단위를 canonical concept로 승격하지 않고 source-system convention을 통합 기준 안에 그대로 허용한 설계 결정. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 number지만 kg 또는 lb를 창고별 관례대로 쓴다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이고 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 저장되므로 단위가 다른 원천 값이 합산 기준으로 들어갈 수 있다. Source finding context: 이 설계는 사용자가 요청한 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험 중심의 적합성 기준을 약화한다.

The problem is root-level because independent status mappings can make current and historical order, shipment, tracking, and displayed states unreproducible. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type, status integration rule Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:19-22,44-49,60-68,111-117; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 상태 모델의 매핑과 최종 표시 권위를 각 연동·운영 판단에 맡기는 결정은 통합 기준 문서의 상태·권위 정합 목적에서 drift를 만든다. Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 즉 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서로서 상태 모델과 권위를 검토해야 한다는 기준이다. 대상은 상태 전이를 통합 의미로 정렬하지 않고 연동별 해석에 위임해, 동일 배송 사건이 주문 상태·화물 상태·추적 이벤트·ETA 표시에서 서로 다른 의미를 가질 수 있게 한다. Source finding context: canonical fulfillment/shipment state model을 두고 각 source status/event의 mapping table, precedence, conflict resolution, freshness, override authority를 명시한다. 운영팀 ETA override는 원천 carrier ETA와 별도 필드로 보존하고 표시값의 결정 규칙과 감사 속성을 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 상태 모델과 권위 정합성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 캐리어 이벤트, TMS shipment 상태, OMS 주문 상태, 운영팀 ETA 수정이 서로 다른 시점이나 의미를 가지며 통합 화면·후속 프로세스에서 함께 사용될 때. Source finding context: 연동별 상태 해석이 분산되면 배송 완료, 예외, 지연, 주문 완료 판단의 기준이 달라져 통합 문서가 운영 의사결정의 기준 역할을 하지 못한다. Source finding context: 상태와 ETA의 canonical authority 및 precedence를 온톨로지에서 정의하지 않고 각 시스템·연동·운영 판단에 분산시킨 설계 결정. Source finding context: Order.fulfillment_status는 OMS가 관리하고 Shipment.status는 별도 TMS 성격의 화물 상태로 정의된다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type은 carrier별 상태 코드이며 자사 Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에서 알아서 한다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: 무결성 규칙은 Order, Shipment, TrackingEvent 상태를 각 시스템이 독립 관리한다고 명시한다. Source finding context: ETA는 carrier API와 운영팀 수기 조정값이 섞이고 최종 표시값이 운영팀 판단에 따른다고 되어 있어 표시 권위와 원천 권위가 분리되지 않는다. Source finding context: 이 조합은 사용자가 요청한 상태 모델·권위 정합성 기준에 대해 통합 기준 문서의 목적 부합성을 낮춘다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type와 Shipment.status 상태 확장 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:44, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:63, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:65, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:66, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:113 Source finding context: 새 운송사 상태 코드나 외부 표준 변경을 수용하려면 enum과 연동별 매핑을 반복 수정해야 하며 상태 의미의 연속성이 보장되지 않는다. Source finding context: 변경 내성의 핵심은 외부 표준이 바뀌어도 기존 이벤트와 규칙의 의미를 보존하는 것이다. 현재 모델은 carrier-native 상태, canonical 상태, 매핑 버전, 적용 기간을 분리하지 않아 상태 변화가 발생하면 enum 확장과 개별 연동 로직 수정이 동시에 필요하다. 그 결과 과거 TrackingEvent가 당시 어떤 매핑 규칙으로 Shipment.status에 영향을 줬는지 재현하기 어렵고, WMS/TMS/ERP 간 상태 통합 규칙도 연동별 구현으로 흩어진다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent에는 carrier_code, native_status_code, native_status_version을 보존하고, 별도 StatusMapping 개념으로 canonical Shipment.status, mapping_version, effective_from/to, precedence를 모델링한다. enum은 내부 canonical 상태로 한정하고 외부 코드는 데이터로 확장 가능하게 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003 Source finding context: TMS/캐리어 상태와 OMS/ERP 상태를 통합할 때 외부 상태 변경에도 안정적인 기준과 이력 재현성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 새 운송사 도입, 캐리어 상태 코드 추가/변경, 예외·반품·부분배송 등 새로운 상태 범주가 추가되는 경우. Source finding context: 상태 enum 또는 각 연동 구현을 직접 바꿔야 하며, 과거 이벤트가 어떤 매핑 기준으로 해석됐는지 온톨로지 수준에서 유지되지 않는다. Source finding context: 외부 상태 코드와 내부 표준 상태의 매핑이 버전 있는 개념으로 모델링되지 않고 고정 enum 및 연동별 재량에 의존한다. Source finding context: Shipment.status는 created, picked, in_transit, delivered의 고정 enum으로 정의된다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type도 고정 enum 값을 갖지만 캐리어별 상태 코드라고 설명된다. Source finding context: Order, Shipment, TrackingEvent 상태는 각 시스템이 독립 관리하고 매핑은 각 연동에서 처리하도록 되어 있어 공통 확장 지점이 없다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-005: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The projection treats this as the main canonical measurement quantity issue. It is root-level because unitless physical quantities can silently corrupt freight, routing, capacity, and historical interpretation when warehouses, carriers, or source systems differ. Source finding context: Quantity and measurement unit coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:28-33, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:35-44 Source finding context: SKU dimensions and weights lack canonical units, while shipment weight is derived from them. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration depends on consistent physical quantities for rating, routing, capacity, labels, and customs-adjacent operations even if international shipment is out of scope. Deriving shipment weight from unitless or source-local measurements creates a coverage gap in the unit dimension of the ontology. Source finding context: Add measurement value objects or attributes for numeric value plus canonical unit, dimensional components, conversion basis, and source unit; require derived shipment totals to specify unit and derivation timestamp. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for SKU, freight, and carrier integration. Source finding context: When different warehouses or systems provide kg/lb or dimension strings with different conventions, shipment totals and carrier-facing measurements cannot be interpreted reliably from the ontology. Source finding context: The model can permit silent unit mixing in operational integration points such as cartonization, carrier rating, routing, and freight reconciliation. Source finding context: Measurement units are not modeled as first-class concepts or required attributes for physical quantities. Source finding context: SKU weight is a number whose unit follows each warehouse's local convention. Source finding context: SKU dimensions are a free-form string whose units vary by source system. Source finding context: Shipment total weight is calculated and stored from order-line weights, inheriting the missing-unit problem. Source finding context: Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight 단위 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:37, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:38, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48 Source finding context: 창고·운송사별 단위 확장 시 기존 중량/치수 값의 의미가 변환 불가능해져 출하 중량 데이터의 연속성이 끊긴다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 단위는 나중에 붙일 표시 정보가 아니라 수량의 의미를 구성하는 권위 정보다. 현재 구조는 단위가 값과 분리되어 있지 않아, lb 창고와 kg 창고를 동시에 운영하거나 운송사가 부피중량/실중량 규칙을 요구하는 변경이 오면 기존 숫자들을 해석·변환·검증할 기준이 없다. 특히 total_weight가 출하 확정 시 저장되는 파생값이므로, 단위 보존 없이 계산된 과거 shipment 데이터는 단위 표준화 이후에도 신뢰 가능한 비교 기준으로 유지되기 어렵다. Source finding context: 중량과 치수를 Quantity 값 객체로 모델링해 value, unit, measurement_system/source, measured_at 또는 calculation_basis를 포함한다. Shipment.total_weight도 구성 SKU 중량의 단순 숫자 합이 아니라 단위 변환 규칙과 계산 버전을 가진 파생 측정값으로 정의한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합에서 SKU, 출하, 운송 요율·제약 판단에 쓰이는 수량 단위의 공통 기준 제공. Source finding context: kg/lb가 혼재된 창고, 다른 치수 단위를 쓰는 소스 시스템, 또는 단위 민감 운송사/요율 규칙을 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 기존 숫자 필드의 단위 의미가 보존되지 않아 신규 단위 체계와 기존 출하 데이터를 같은 기준으로 변환하거나 감사하기 어렵다. Source finding context: 측정값이 단위와 계산 기준을 포함한 독립 개념이 아니라 number/string 필드에 소스 관례로 저장된다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 숫자이며 창고별 계량 관례인 kg 또는 lb를 그대로 쓴다고 정의된다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이고 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다고 정의된다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 저장되지만 단위 또는 계산 기준 필드가 없다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-006: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The issue is supported as an operational trust and coverage gap. It is root-level for auditability because representing only mutable final values loses the evidence needed to explain or challenge customer-facing ETA and inventory-truth changes. Source finding context: Manual and reconciliation audit evidence coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:44, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:76-79, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:110-112 Source finding context: Manual ETA overrides and inventory reconciliation adjustments lack audit evidence concepts. Source finding context: The coverage lens asks whether controlled actions have evidence for actor, time, and basis. Manual ETA changes and inventory reconciliation directly affect customer communication and stock truth, so a concept baseline that omits audit evidence cannot support accountability or dispute analysis. Source finding context: Add audit/control entities such as `ManualOverride`, `InventoryAdjustment`, or `OperationalDecision` with actor, occurred_at, source value, prior value, new value, reason, approval/reference evidence, and affected entity references. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: Use as an operationally trustworthy WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline. Source finding context: When an ETA or inventory value changes due to manual judgment or batch reconciliation, the ontology cannot represent who changed it, when, from what evidence, or why. Source finding context: Operational risk increases because customer-facing and accounting/warehouse reconciliation changes cannot be audited or challenged from the shared model. Source finding context: The ontology represents final mutable values but omits control-action evidence concepts for manual and batch changes. Source finding context: Shipment ETA can be adjusted manually by the field operations team. Source finding context: The final displayed ETA mixes carrier API values with manual operation-team judgment. Source finding context: Inventory quantities are held separately by WMS and ERP and adjusted through nightly reconciliation, but no adjustment evidence concept is modeled.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-007: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The projection shows consistent support that cancellation, failure, return, correction, and exception meanings are absent. This is a root coverage issue because integrations otherwise overload happy-path or external states for materially different outcomes. Source finding context: Order and shipment lifecycle coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:14-17, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:39-42, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:55-63 Source finding context: The lifecycle states cover only the happy path and omit cancellation, failure, return, correction, and post-delivery exception handling. Source finding context: Fulfillment operations require non-happy-path and post-terminal coverage: cancellations, failed delivery, loss/damage, return-to-sender, returns, redelivery, proof-of-delivery correction, and reopened exceptions. Without those categories, the concept model cannot express common operational outcomes or distinguish final completion from later correction. Source finding context: Extend lifecycle models with explicit non-happy-path and post-delivery states/events, such as canceled, delivery_failed, exception_open, exception_resolved, return_initiated, returned, corrected/reissued, and define how tracking events transition or annotate shipment/order state. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-005 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for order, shipment, and delivery state models. Source finding context: When an order or shipment is canceled, fails delivery, returns, receives a carrier exception, or needs post-delivery correction, the ontology has no lifecycle category to represent the outcome without overloading `delivered` or external handling. Source finding context: Integrations can diverge on state interpretation and cannot consistently model operational risk events that materially affect customer service, inventory, and settlement. Source finding context: Lifecycle coverage is limited to forward fulfillment states and does not include exception resolution or terminal correction concepts. Source finding context: Order fulfillment status progresses from received to delivered with no cancellation, return, failure, or correction states. Source finding context: Shipment status progresses from created to delivered and notes that delivered is final. Source finding context: Tracking events can report `exception`, but no shipment/order lifecycle state or resolution concept corresponds to that event.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-008: fix_now
  - rationale: The projection shows evolution, logic, semantics, structure, and axiology support. The inheritance choice is a root classification error because leg lifecycle, carrier, ETA, status, and fulfillment scope differ from whole-shipment scope. Source finding context: Shipment와 DeliveryLeg 상속 구조 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:41, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:52, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:54, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:105, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:106, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:107, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:108 Source finding context: 다구간·환적·역물류 확장 시 배송 구간이 전체 화물의 속성과 주문 충족 관계를 상속해 경로 모델을 수정해야 한다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 전체 Shipment와 구간 DeliveryLeg는 lifecycle, 상태, 운송사, ETA, 주문 충족 책임이 다르게 진화하는 축이다. 현재는 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment를 상속하므로 구간이 전체 shipment의 fulfills 관계와 total_weight 의미에 끌려 들어간다. 다구간 TMS 통합이 들어오면 구간별 carrier/status/ETA는 필요하지만 구간이 주문 전체를 충족한다고 볼 수 없는 경우가 생겨 기존 구조가 확장보다 재분류를 요구한다. Source finding context: Shipment를 전체 consignment/fulfillment movement로 두고 DeliveryLeg는 상속이 아니라 route component 관계로 연결한다. ShipmentRoute 또는 LegAssignment를 추가해 leg_seq, from/to, carrier, leg_status, leg_eta를 구간 속성으로 분리하고, fulfills는 전체 Shipment 또는 별도 FulfillmentAllocation에만 두도록 범위를 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004 Source finding context: TMS 통합에서 전체 화물, 배송 구간, 주문 충족 관계를 확장 가능한 개념 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 복수 구간, 복수 운송사, 허브 환적, 구간별 상태/ETA 또는 반품 경로를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 구간 엔티티가 전체 Shipment 의미를 상속해 신규 경로 유형을 기존 구조 수정 없이 추가하기 어렵고, 주문 충족 관계가 구간 단위로 잘못 확장될 위험이 있다. Source finding context: 전체 화물 이동과 경로 구간이 상속 관계로 결합되어 각자의 독립적인 lifecycle과 관계 범위가 분리되지 않는다. Source finding context: Shipment는 창고에서 고객으로 가는 물리적 화물 이동으로 정의되고 DeliveryLeg도 Shipment의 일종이라고 설명된다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 is_a: Shipment로 선언되어 전체 Shipment의 status, total_weight, eta, carrier_ref 구조를 상속한다. Source finding context: fulfills 관계는 Shipment에서 Order로 정의되어 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment 하위형인 한 구간에도 주문 충족 관계가 적용될 수 있다. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment hierarchy Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:35-49 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is declared as a Shipment even though its own definition allows segments that do not satisfy the Shipment definition. Source finding context: The conflict is inter-claim: the superclass definition at lines 35-36 imposes a warehouse-to-customer movement shape, and the subclass definition at lines 47-49 allows partial route segments. This makes at least the example leg instances unsatisfiable as Shipment instances without weakening either the Shipment definition or the subtype assertion. For a WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline, this also propagates Shipment-level status, carrier, and fulfillment relations onto leg-level objects whose lifecycle is not necessarily equivalent to an end-to-end shipment. Source finding context: Separate end-to-end `Shipment` from `DeliveryLeg` composition, for example model `Shipment` has many `DeliveryLeg`s instead of `DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment`; or broaden `Shipment` to a generic transport movement and introduce a distinct end-to-end shipment concept for warehouse-to-customer fulfillment. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, cargo, and delivery. Source finding context: When a delivery route contains warehouse-to-hub or hub-to-customer legs, those DeliveryLeg instances must both be Shipment instances and not satisfy the stated Shipment definition. Source finding context: The ontology cannot consistently classify leg-level and shipment-level records, weakening integration rules that rely on inherited Shipment status, carrier, and fulfillment semantics. Source finding context: The model uses subtype inheritance for a part-of route segment relationship. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is declared with `is_a: Shipment`. Source finding context: `Shipment` is defined as an end-to-end warehouse-to-customer movement. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is defined as a route segment and explicitly includes warehouse-to-hub and hub-to-customer examples, which are partial movements rather than each being warehouse-to-customer movements.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-009: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: Deliberation narrowed this issue under the DeliveryLeg inheritance/composition cluster. It preserves an important semantic consequence, but the underlying root is already captured by issue-008 and related composition work. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg.is_a Shipment Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40,52,54,106 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 하위유형으로 둔 것은 구간과 전체 화물 이동의 의미를 혼동한다. Source finding context: 구간은 전체 출하/화물의 구성 요소이지 동일한 존재론적 유형이 아니다. 특히 허브행 구간은 고객에게 가는 전체 Shipment가 아니며, Shipment.status의 delivered 최종 상태와 DeliveryLeg의 구간 도착 완료 상태도 같은 의미가 아니다. WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서에서 이 상속은 출하 단위 추적, 구간 상태, 주문 충족 여부의 의미를 섞어 시스템 간 매핑 오류를 만들 수 있다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 하위유형이 아니라 ShipmentRoute 또는 ShipmentLeg 같은 구성 요소로 모델링하고, Shipment가 여러 DeliveryLeg를 갖는 관계를 둔다. 구간 상태와 전체 Shipment 상태를 별도 enum으로 분리하고, fulfills 관계는 전체 Shipment 또는 실제 주문라인 충족 단위에만 붙인다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 개념을 일관되게 구분하는 목적. Source finding context: 다구간 배송에서 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment로 취급되어 구간 단위가 주문 충족 단위 또는 전체 출하 단위로 해석될 때. Source finding context: TMS의 leg 상태와 OMS/ERP의 shipment fulfillment 상태가 같은 개념처럼 매핑되어 배송 완료, 운임, 주문 충족 판단의 기준이 흔들린다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg의 존재론적 유형을 Shipment의 하위유형으로 선언했다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 경로의 한 구간으로 정의되어 전체 창고-고객 이동인 Shipment와 다른 의미를 가진다. Source finding context: 상속 때문에 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment의 속성과 fulfills(Order) 관계까지 받는 완전한 Shipment처럼 해석된다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-010: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: The resolution keeps this as a narrower semantic-authority split within issue-001. It is a concrete symptom of the broader inventory authority and temporality defect rather than the primary root by itself. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76,81,83,116 Source finding context: quantity_on_hand가 실물 재고와 회계 재고라는 서로 다른 의미를 한 속성명에 합친다. Source finding context: '현재 보유 수량'이라는 단일 이름은 물리적으로 창고에 있는 수량과 회계/ERP 기준 수량을 같은 의미로 보이게 만든다. 두 값은 권위, 사용 목적, 조정 시점이 다르므로 같은 속성으로 두면 통합 기준 문서가 어떤 값을 재고 진실로 삼는지 모호해진다. Source finding context: quantity_on_hand를 physical_on_hand_qty(WMS authority)와 accounting_inventory_qty 또는 erp_book_qty(ERP authority)처럼 의미와 권위를 분리한다. 각 속성에 source_system, authority, as_of/reconciled_at을 붙이고 조정 결과는 별도 reconciliation 개념으로 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: 재고 개념의 권위와 상태를 WMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 명확히 하는 목적. Source finding context: 재고 조회, 할당, 회계 조정에서 quantity_on_hand 하나를 WMS 실물 수량 또는 ERP 회계 수량으로 상황별 해석할 때. Source finding context: 같은 필드가 서로 다른 재고 의미를 대표하여 가용성 판단과 회계 수량 조정의 기준이 충돌한다. Source finding context: 서로 다른 권위와 의미를 가진 WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고를 quantity_on_hand라는 단일 속성으로 표현했다. Source finding context: quantity_on_hand는 현재 보유 수량으로 정의된다. Source finding context: 동일 설명 안에서 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가지며 기준도 실물과 회계로 다르다고 밝힌다.

- issue-011: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: Deliberation consolidated this into issue-005 while preserving its semantic requirements. It is a symptom/facet of the canonical measurement root because the same remediation cluster addresses unit meaning and conversion history. Source finding context: Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33,37,38,48 Source finding context: 중량·치수 속성이 단위를 의미의 일부로 고정하지 않아 같은 이름이 kg/lb와 소스별 치수 체계를 동시에 뜻한다. Source finding context: weight와 dims는 수치만으로 의미가 완성되지 않고 단위가 포함되어야 한다. 단위 없는 'weight'와 문자열 'dims'는 시스템별로 다른 의미를 같은 속성명 아래에 숨기며, total_weight 같은 파생값은 서로 다른 단위가 합산될 위험이 있다. Source finding context: weight_value와 weight_uom, dimension_value 구조(length/width/height)와 dimension_uom을 분리하거나 표준 단위로 정규화한다. total_weight에는 계산 기준 단위와 계산 시점, 원천 SKU/OrderLine 단위 변환 규칙을 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 단위 정합성을 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 여러 창고나 소스 시스템의 SKU가 한 Shipment에 포함되거나 total_weight가 운임/적재/배송 제한 판단에 사용될 때. Source finding context: 동일 숫자가 kg 또는 lb로 해석될 수 있어 중량 합계와 치수 기반 운영 판단이 의미적으로 신뢰 불가능해진다. Source finding context: 측정값 속성의 의미를 구성하는 단위를 별도 권위 있는 속성이나 표준 단위로 모델링하지 않았다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 kg 또는 lb를 창고별 관례대로 사용한다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이며 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 원천 중량들의 합으로 저장되어 단위 혼합이 파생값 의미까지 전파된다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-012: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: The resolution preserves this as an available_qty-specific currentness and stale-snapshot risk linked to issue-001. It is a concrete symptom of missing inventory freshness, traceability, and live allocatable authority. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86,90,112 Source finding context: available_qty라는 이름이 현재 가용 재고를 뜻하지만 실제로는 야간 스냅샷 값이다. Source finding context: 'available'은 주문 시점의 할당 가능 수량처럼 읽히지만, 모델의 설명은 전일 또는 야간 배치 시점의 스냅샷에 가깝다. 이름과 시간성이 어긋나면 OMS/WMS/ERP가 같은 값을 현재 가용 재고로 오해할 수 있다. Source finding context: available_qty_snapshot, snapshot_as_of 같은 시간성 있는 이름과 속성으로 바꾸거나, 현재 할당 가능 수량은 실시간 계산 개념으로 분리한다. 일중 할당 규칙은 어떤 시점의 재고를 권위로 삼는지 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004 Source finding context: 주문·재고 통합에서 재고 가용성의 의미와 시간 기준을 명확히 하는 목적. Source finding context: 일중 주문 할당이 nightly snapshot available_qty를 현재 가용량으로 사용할 때. Source finding context: 재고 할당 기준이 현재 상태인지 스냅샷인지 불분명해 과할당 또는 보수적 미할당 같은 운영 위험을 낳는다. Source finding context: 시간성 있는 스냅샷 값을 현재성을 암시하는 available_qty 이름으로 모델링했다. Source finding context: available_qty는 매일 밤 배치로 저장되는 스냅샷으로 설명된다. Source finding context: 동시에 일중 주문 할당도 이 값을 읽는다고 규칙화되어 현재 가용량처럼 사용된다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-015: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Deliberation resolved this as distinct from issue-008: issue-008 covers wrong inheritance semantics, while issue-015 covers absent ordered route composition and traceability for multi-leg reconstruction and leg-localized tracking. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Shipment-DeliveryLeg route structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40-58,105-109 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is typed as a Shipment but no structure connects an end-to-end Shipment to its legs. Source finding context: An is_a link says a DeliveryLeg is a kind of Shipment; it does not connect a multi-leg shipment to the ordered sequence of legs that compose its route. For a delivery concept model, this leaves the physical delivery path disconnected from the shipment it is supposed to detail. Source finding context: Introduce an explicit composition/sequence relation, for example Shipment has_many DeliveryLeg ordered by leg_seq, or a Route entity containing DeliveryLeg instances, and define whether TrackingEvent attaches to the whole Shipment, a leg, or both. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002 Source finding context: Using the ontology as a concept baseline for shipment and delivery integration. Source finding context: When a shipment has multiple delivery legs, the model cannot reconstruct which legs belong to the shipment or the ordered route for that shipment. Source finding context: TMS/carrier integrations cannot reliably connect route segments, locations, and tracking events back to the end-to-end shipment path using the ontology structure. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is modeled only through inheritance from Shipment, without a composition relation from a shipment or route to its legs. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg has leg_seq, from_ref, and to_ref, indicating a route segment, but no parent shipment or route reference. Source finding context: The only structural connection involving Shipment in the relations section is fulfills from Shipment to Order. Source finding context: TrackingEvent points to Shipment generally, so without a shipment-leg composition path events cannot be structurally localized to a route segment when needed.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-016: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: The projection narrows this as a structural traceability facet under issue-001. It enables the broader inventory authority problem by making allocation quantities unreadable as auditable derivations from source records and metadata. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / InventoryAggregate source structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90,111-116 Source finding context: InventoryAggregate is not structurally connected to the InventoryRecord records it aggregates. Source finding context: The ontology declares InventoryAggregate as the quantity used for allocation, including intraday orders, and notes that WMS and ERP each hold inventory truth that is reconciled by nightly batch. Without an explicit aggregate-from/source relation, the inventory path from warehouse-level records to enterprise availability is structurally incomplete. Source finding context: Add an explicit derivation or aggregation relation from InventoryAggregate to its source InventoryRecord set, including scope such as warehouse set, source system/authority, and snapshot/as_of metadata if the aggregate is batch-produced. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003 Source finding context: Using the ontology as the inventory concept baseline for WMS/ERP allocation and reconciliation. Source finding context: When allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty, the model cannot trace the aggregate back to warehouse-level inventory records or their source systems. Source finding context: Consumers can read the allocation quantity but cannot verify, reconcile, or route disputes through the ontology structure, weakening trust in cross-system inventory integration. Source finding context: The aggregate inventory concept is represented as a standalone SKU quantity without a structural derivation path from warehouse inventory records or inventory authorities. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate has only sku_ref and available_qty; it has no reference to InventoryRecord, Warehouse, WMS, ERP, or a source snapshot. Source finding context: InventoryRecord is the warehouse/SKU inventory record, but no relation connects it to InventoryAggregate. Source finding context: The allocation rule relies on InventoryAggregate.available_qty, making the disconnected aggregate a live structural dependency for order fulfillment.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-013: needs_evidence
  - rationale: The projection explicitly classifies this as low severity and non-material within the bounded evidence. It should be watched or revisited only if tied to displayed-state authority, override audit, or operational decision failures. Source finding context: Shipment.eta Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:49,117 Source finding context: eta가 캐리어 ETA와 운영팀 조정 ETA를 한 값으로 합쳐 예측값의 출처 의미를 잃는다. Source finding context: 캐리어가 제공한 ETA와 운영팀이 표시용으로 조정한 ETA는 출처와 사용 목적이 다르다. 단일 eta 속성은 원천 예측, 운영 보정, 최종 표시값을 같은 의미로 보이게 하므로 TMS/캐리어/고객 표시 간 의미 매핑이 흐려진다. Source finding context: carrier_eta, ops_adjusted_eta, displayed_eta를 분리하거나 eta 값에 source, authority, adjusted_by, adjusted_at을 명시한다. 최종 표시값이 운영팀 판단이라면 그 파생 규칙을 별도 속성 또는 규칙으로 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-005

### Consensus (6/6, core-axis mode)
- issue-001 (high): Inventory authority and temporality must be closed in the ontology before it can serve as a reliable WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline. The current model lets live allocation, WMS/ERP reconciliation, and future real-time inventory extension depend on current or nightly stock numbers whose source authority, effective time, and allocation basis are not explicit.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: logic
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 3/3 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution
  - remaining disagreement: 0/3 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution
  - issue statement: Inventory authority and temporality are not closed enough for allocation, reconciliation, and later real-time inventory extension. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / InventoryRecord, InventoryAggregate, inventory allocation rule, inventory truth note Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90,111-116; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 재고 권위와 시간성을 야간 ERP 정합 및 일별 스냅샷에 맞추는 결정이 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서라는 목적과 어긋난다. Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 즉 이 온톨로지가 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로 적절해야 하며 특히 권위와 시간성을 검토해야 한다는 기준이다. 대상은 여러 시스템의 재고 진실이 갈리는 지점을 통합 기준으로 닫지 않고, 운영상 빠르게 변하는 주문 할당을 야간 ERP 스냅샷으로 처리하도록 두어 목적상 중요한 재고 가용성 판단을 약화한다. Source finding context: 재고 개념에 system_of_record/source_authority, as_of/effective_at, snapshot_generated_at, reservation/allocation 이벤트를 추가하고, 일중 할당은 현재 가용 재고나 명시된 reservation ledger를 권위로 삼도록 기준을 분리한다. ERP 회계 수량 정합은 별도 reconciliation 상태와 시점으로 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·재고 개념의 권위와 시간성 정합성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 일중 주문, 부분 재고 변동, WMS/ERP 불일치 상황에서 allocation이 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty나 권위가 불명확한 quantity_on_hand를 기준으로 판단될 때. Source finding context: 통합 기준 문서가 어느 시스템의 어느 시점 재고를 믿어야 하는지 닫지 못해 과할당, 품절 오판, 사후 회계 정합과 물리 재고 운영의 혼선을 유발할 수 있다. Source finding context: 재고 권위와 시간성을 온톨로지의 필수 개념으로 닫지 않고 야간 ERP 정합 및 스냅샷 사용을 운영 규칙으로 수용한 설계 결정. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand는 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가지며 야간 배치로 ERP 기준에 맞춘다고 정의된다. Source finding context: InventoryRecord에는 as_of가 없어 주문 접수 당시 재고 같은 시점 질의가 불가능하다고 대상 자체가 명시한다. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 매일 밤 스냅샷인데, 일중 주문 재고 할당도 이 값을 읽도록 규칙화되어 있다. Source finding context: 이 조합은 사용자가 요구한 권위·시간성 중심의 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 목적을 약화한다. Source finding context: Inventory temporal and allocation coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:71-85, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:106-107 Source finding context: Inventory is modeled as current and nightly aggregate quantities without effective time, history, or reservation/allocation events. Source finding context: Inventory in fulfillment is time-dependent. Without temporal validity and allocation events, the model cannot reconstruct stock at order placement, explain oversell/short-pick conditions, or distinguish physical quantity, available-to-promise, reserved quantity, and accounting quantity. Source finding context: Introduce inventory event or snapshot concepts with `as_of`/effective interval, source system, quantity type, and reason; add reservation/allocation records that connect order lines to inventory at a specific time and location. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for inventory allocation and WMS/ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: When intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, or nightly reconciliations occur, the ontology cannot reconstruct the inventory basis used for allocation or audit a later discrepancy. Source finding context: Allocation decisions can be impossible to justify after the fact, and WMS/ERP quantity differences cannot be traced through a shared conceptual model. Source finding context: Inventory coverage stops at current quantity and daily aggregate values rather than modeling time-indexed inventory facts and allocation events. Source finding context: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` is described as a current value held independently by WMS and ERP. Source finding context: The ontology comment says there is no effective-time concept for reconstructing inventory at order receipt. Source finding context: `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` is a nightly snapshot, yet intraday allocation reads it. Source finding context: InventoryRecord / InventoryAggregate 재고 시간성 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:81, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:90, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:112, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:116 Source finding context: 일중/실시간 재고 확장 시 기존 재고 구조에 시간·출처·스냅샷 권위 필드 추가가 필요해 기존 데이터 연속성이 깨진다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 문제는 단순히 현재 as_of가 없다는 표면 결함이 아니라, 일중 주문 처리라는 현재 사용 경로가 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty에 직접 결합되어 있다는 점이다. 새 재고 소스나 더 빠른 동기화 주기를 추가하면 같은 available_qty 숫자가 어느 시점·어느 권위·어느 계산 기준의 값인지 구분할 수 없으므로, 과거 주문의 당시 재고 판단과 신규 실시간 판단을 같은 구조 안에서 함께 유지하기 어렵다. WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로 쓰일 경우 재고 할당의 변경 이력이 추적되지 않아 운영상 과할당/미할당 원인 분석과 단계적 마이그레이션이 약해진다. Source finding context: InventoryQuantity를 시간·권위·목적이 있는 개념으로 분리하고, 최소한 source_system, authority_basis, as_of/effective_at, snapshot_id 또는 calculation_version을 추가한다. InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 원천 수량이 아니라 특정 계산 시점의 파생 스냅샷으로 명시하고, 할당 규칙은 읽는 재고 뷰의 freshness/authority 요구를 참조하도록 바꾼다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문 접수, 재고 할당, 출하 판단의 권위와 시간성을 안정적으로 정의하는 목적. Source finding context: 야간 배치 기반 집계에서 일중·실시간 재고 이벤트, 복수 WMS/ERP 출처, 예약 재고 또는 과거 시점 조회를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 기존 available_qty/quantity_on_hand 값이 어떤 권위와 시점의 값인지 보존하지 못해 기존 데이터와 신규 데이터의 의미가 갈라지고, 통합 규칙을 기존 구조 수정 없이 확장하기 어렵다. Source finding context: 재고 수량 개념이 현재 숫자 값과 야간 집계 결과에 고정되어 있고, 시간·출처·계산 버전이 개념 모델의 일급 속성으로 분리되어 있지 않다. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand는 현재 보유 수량으로만 정의되고 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가진다고 설명된다. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty는 매일 밤 배치로 저장되는 스냅샷인데, 일중 주문 할당도 이 값을 읽도록 규칙이 고정되어 있다. Source finding context: WMS 실물 기준과 ERP 회계 기준의 불일치는 야간 배치 조정으로만 설명되어, 새 재고 출처나 실시간 동기화 주기를 기존 의미와 병행할 확장 지점이 없다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for inventory allocation, WMS/ERP reconciliation, order receipt, and shipment decisions. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·재고 개념의 권위와 시간성 정합성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for inventory allocation and WMS/ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문 접수, 재고 할당, 출하 판단의 권위와 시간성을 안정적으로 정의하는 목적.
  - failure condition: Intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, nightly reconciliations, multiple WMS/ERP sources, or historical inventory lookup depend on quantity_on_hand or nightly InventoryAggregate.available_qty as the allocation basis. Source finding context: 일중 주문, 부분 재고 변동, WMS/ERP 불일치 상황에서 allocation이 야간 InventoryAggregate.available_qty나 권위가 불명확한 quantity_on_hand를 기준으로 판단될 때. Source finding context: When intraday orders, stock changes, reservations, or nightly reconciliations occur, the ontology cannot reconstruct the inventory basis used for allocation or audit a later discrepancy. Source finding context: 야간 배치 기반 집계에서 일중·실시간 재고 이벤트, 복수 WMS/ERP 출처, 예약 재고 또는 과거 시점 조회를 추가하는 경우.
  - impact: The ontology cannot state which system and time basis a stock value represents, making allocation decisions hard to justify, WMS/ERP differences hard to audit, and real-time inventory evolution disruptive. Source finding context: 통합 기준 문서가 어느 시스템의 어느 시점 재고를 믿어야 하는지 닫지 못해 과할당, 품절 오판, 사후 회계 정합과 물리 재고 운영의 혼선을 유발할 수 있다. Source finding context: Allocation decisions can be impossible to justify after the fact, and WMS/ERP quantity differences cannot be traced through a shared conceptual model. Source finding context: 기존 available_qty/quantity_on_hand 값이 어떤 권위와 시점의 값인지 보존하지 못해 기존 데이터와 신규 데이터의 의미가 갈라지고, 통합 규칙을 기존 구조 수정 없이 확장하기 어렵다.
  - root hypothesis: Inventory availability is modeled as current/nightly quantities without first-class authority, effective time, source qualification, or allocation/reservation events.

`issue-ledger.yaml#dep-002`, `rel-004`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-003`, `rel-005`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-004`, `rel-006`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose because inventory allocation, order receipt, shipment decisions, and WMS/ERP reconciliation all require knowing which inventory value is authoritative and when it applies. If intraday allocation reads a nightly InventoryAggregate.available_qty or an ambiguous InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand, the baseline cannot justify allocation decisions, reconstruct stock at order time, or audit later WMS/ERP discrepancies.
  - root cause: The starting point is that inventory availability is modeled as current/nightly quantities rather than as time- and authority-qualified inventory facts. Because source authority, effective/as-of time, source qualification, quantity purpose, and reservation/allocation events are not first-class concepts, every downstream use inherits the same ambiguity about what a quantity means and whether it is valid for live allocation, accounting reconciliation, or historical audit.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported across axiology, coverage, and evolution findings. InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand is treated as a current value that WMS and ERP may each hold, with mismatch resolved by nightly ERP adjustment; the model also states there is no effective-time concept for reconstructing inventory at order receipt. InventoryAggregate.available_qty is a nightly snapshot, yet intraday allocation reads it. Together, those choices make a nightly or authority-unclear quantity stand in for live operational availability, which prevents reliable allocation explanation, discrepancy tracing, and continuity when adding real-time inventory sources or reservation ledgers.
  - action: Separate inventory quantity concepts by authority, purpose, source system, effective/as-of time, snapshot or calculation basis, and allocation/reservation events. Treat nightly ERP reconciliation as its own reconciliation state and timestamped accounting alignment, not as the live allocation authority. Allocation rules should reference an explicitly authorized and fresh inventory view or reservation ledger, so physical availability, available-to-promise, reserved quantity, and accounting quantity can evolve without changing the meaning of existing records.

- issue-004 (high): Partial, split, substituted, backordered, or exception fulfillment cannot be represented at the order-line quantity grain because fulfillment is modeled only from Shipment to Order, not from shipment or allocation activity to specific OrderLine quantities.

  - issue statement: Partial, split, substituted, backordered, or line-level shipment fulfillment cannot be represented at the order-line quantity grain. Source finding context: OrderLine fulfillment coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:18, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104 Source finding context: OrderLine-level fulfillment mapping is absent, so partial shipments cannot be represented at the line level. Source finding context: For a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline, fulfillment coverage needs to answer which SKU quantity on which order line was picked, shipped, split, backordered, or delivered. An order-level `fulfills` relation collapses those distinctions and makes line-level reconciliation across OMS, WMS, TMS, and ERP unavailable. Source finding context: Add a line-level fulfillment/allocation concept such as `FulfillmentLine` or `ShipmentLine` with `order_line_ref`, `shipment_ref`, fulfilled quantity, shipped quantity, and status; keep the order-level relation as a derived aggregate if still needed. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, inventory allocation, shipment, and delivery. Source finding context: When an order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, or backorder, the ontology cannot state which order-line quantity moved in which shipment or leg. Source finding context: Operational reconciliation and exception handling become non-actionable because the integration model cannot tie physical movement and delivery evidence back to the commercial order line. Source finding context: The ontology only defines shipment-to-order fulfillment and omits a fulfillment concept at the order-line quantity grain. Source finding context: `Order` contains a list of `OrderLine` objects, but `OrderLine` only has SKU and quantity attributes. Source finding context: The only fulfillment relation is `Shipment -> Order`, explicitly at order grain rather than order-line grain. Source finding context: The target comment states that partial-shipment tracing from line to shipment is not possible in the ontology.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline for order, inventory allocation, shipment, delivery, reconciliation, and exception handling. Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, inventory allocation, shipment, and delivery.
  - failure condition: An order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, backorder, or line-level delivery exception. Source finding context: When an order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, or backorder, the ontology cannot state which order-line quantity moved in which shipment or leg.
  - impact: The model cannot tie physical movement and delivery evidence back to the commercial order line, making reconciliation and exceptions non-actionable. Source finding context: Operational reconciliation and exception handling become non-actionable because the integration model cannot tie physical movement and delivery evidence back to the commercial order line.
  - root hypothesis: Fulfillment is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity, while OrderLine has no shipment, leg, or allocation fulfillment edge.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:18`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:18, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-004`, `finding-004.cause-001`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-24`, `finding-004.cause-002`, `finding-004.cause-003`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:25-26`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This materially weakens the WMS/TMS/ERP baseline because reconciliation and exception handling depend on knowing which SKU quantity on which commercial order line was picked, shipped, delivered, backordered, substituted, or cancelled. Order-level fulfillment collapses those distinctions, so physical movement and delivery evidence cannot be tied back to the obligations that operations and finance need to reconcile.
  - root cause: The root cause starts at the ontology grain: Order has OrderLine objects, but OrderLine carries only SKU and quantity attributes and has no fulfillment, shipment, leg, package, or allocation edge. Since the only fulfillment relation is Shipment -> Order, every downstream fulfillment fact is attached to the order aggregate rather than to the line quantity that was actually fulfilled or not fulfilled.
  - causal path: The causal path is direct: OrderLine exists as the commercial quantity unit, but it is not structurally connected to fulfillment execution; Shipment fulfills only Order; therefore a multi-line order with partial shipment, split shipment, substitution, cancellation, backorder, or line-level delivery exception cannot state which line quantity moved in which shipment or leg. The deliberation accepted this coverage gap without further dispute: coverage raised it, other applicable lenses supported it, and logic was only not applicable because the issue is missing coverage rather than an inconsistency.
  - action: Add a line-level fulfillment or allocation concept, such as FulfillmentLine or ShipmentLine, that connects OrderLine to Shipment and, where needed, DeliveryLeg or package evidence. It should carry fulfilled quantity, shipped quantity, and fulfillment status so order-level fulfillment can remain only as a derived aggregate after the line-level truth exists.

Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / OrderLine-Shipment fulfillment structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:23,25-31,40-50,105-109 Source finding context: OrderLine is not structurally connected to the Shipment that fulfills it. Source finding context: The evidence explicitly states that OrderLine has no reference to the shipment/leg that fulfilled it, while the declared relation deliberately remains at order level. Because the ontology is meant to be a WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept standard, this missing edge breaks a core path between order detail and physical shipment detail, especially for partial shipments or split fulfillment. Source finding context: Add an explicit line-level fulfillment structure, such as ShipmentLine or FulfillmentAllocation, relating OrderLine to Shipment with fulfilled_qty and, if needed, DeliveryLeg/package references. Keep the order-level fulfills relation as a derived or summary relation if useful. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001 Source finding context: Using the ontology as the concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery integration across WMS/TMS/ERP. Source finding context: When one order has multiple lines, partial fulfillment, split shipments, or line-level exceptions, the model cannot identify which OrderLine quantity was fulfilled by which Shipment or route segment. Source finding context: Integration consumers cannot reconcile order-line fulfillment with shipment execution, shipment weight composition, delivery status, or exceptions from the target ontology itself. Source finding context: The fulfillment relation is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity, while OrderLine is left without a shipment/leg fulfillment edge. Source finding context: OrderLine only contains sku_ref and qty, with no attribute or relation to Shipment, DeliveryLeg, or a fulfillment allocation entity. Source finding context: The sole declared fulfillment relation is Shipment -> Order and its note says OrderLine-level mapping is not included. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight depends on 구성 OrderLine weights, so the target already assumes a structural connection that is not represented.

  - impact: Consumers cannot reconcile order-line fulfillment with shipment execution, shipment weight composition, delivery status, or exceptions from the ontology itself. Source finding context: Integration consumers cannot reconcile order-line fulfillment with shipment execution, shipment weight composition, delivery status, or exceptions from the target ontology itself.
  - root hypothesis: The fulfillment relation is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity while OrderLine lacks a shipment or leg fulfillment edge.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:23`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:25-31`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:105-109`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:23,25-31,40-50,105-109`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-019`, `finding-019.cause-001`, `finding-019.cause-002`, `finding-019.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-014`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_now, follow_up
  - materiality: This materially weakens the declared WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline because order-line fulfillment is a core reconciliation path between commercial order detail and shipment execution. Under multiple lines, partial fulfillment, split shipments, or line-level exceptions, consumers cannot determine line-level fulfillment status, shipment composition, delivery progress, or exception responsibility from the ontology itself.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts at the fulfillment grain: the modeled fulfillment relation exists only from Shipment to Order, while OrderLine has no relation or attribute connecting it to Shipment, DeliveryLeg, package, or a fulfillment allocation entity. That order-level-only structure prevents the model from carrying fulfillment facts at the line quantity level.
  - causal path: OrderLine contains only sku_ref and qty, so it cannot identify the shipment or leg that fulfilled any quantity. The sole fulfillment relation is Shipment -> Order and explicitly excludes OrderLine-level mapping. At the same time, Shipment.total_weight depends on composed OrderLine weights, so the ontology already assumes a line-to-shipment composition that it does not represent. Together, those steps produce the observed gap: line-level fulfillment cannot be reconciled to shipment execution.
  - action: Add an explicit line-level fulfillment structure, such as ShipmentLine or FulfillmentAllocation, relating OrderLine to Shipment with fulfilled_qty and optional DeliveryLeg or package references. Keep the existing order-level fulfills relation only as a derived or summary relation if useful, because the line-level allocation must be the authoritative structure for partial, split, and exception workflows.

- issue-002 (medium): issue-002는 소스 시스템별 중량·치수 단위 관례를 그대로 허용하는 설계가 물류 통합 기준 문서의 운영 위험 감소 목적을 약화한다는 material issue이다. 다만 독립 remediation root라기보다 issue-005/issue-011의 canonical measurement-unit cluster에 속하는 source-convention/value-alignment 증상으로 유지되어야 한다.

Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33-49; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 특히 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험 검토 요청이다. 총중량, 운임, 적재, 라벨, carrier handoff에 쓰일 수 있는 값을 단위 없는 number/string으로 합산 가능하게 두면 통합 기준이 운영 위험을 예방하기보다 각 연동의 관례 차이를 그대로 전파한다. Source finding context: weight와 dims를 수치 값과 canonical unit으로 분리하고, 원천 단위와 변환 시점/방법을 보존한다. Shipment.total_weight는 canonical unit 기반의 계산 결과와 계산 기준 시점을 함께 갖도록 하며, 단위 미확정 값은 출하 확정 계산에 사용할 수 없게 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험을 다루는 목적. Source finding context: 서로 다른 창고 또는 소스 시스템의 kg/lb, 치수 문자열이 Shipment.total_weight나 배송 연동 값으로 합산·전달될 때. Source finding context: 단위 불일치가 운임 산정, carrier 인계, 적재 계획, 배송 예외 처리의 오류로 이어질 수 있는데, 기준 문서가 이를 막는 공통 의미를 제공하지 못한다. Source finding context: 물류 수량 단위를 canonical concept로 승격하지 않고 source-system convention을 통합 기준 안에 그대로 허용한 설계 결정. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 number지만 kg 또는 lb를 창고별 관례대로 쓴다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이고 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 저장되므로 단위가 다른 원천 값이 합산 기준으로 들어갈 수 있다. Source finding context: 이 설계는 사용자가 요청한 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험 중심의 적합성 기준을 약화한다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험을 다루는 목적.
  - failure condition: 서로 다른 창고 또는 소스 시스템의 kg/lb, 치수 문자열이 Shipment.total_weight나 배송 연동 값으로 합산·전달될 때.
  - impact: 단위 불일치가 운임 산정, carrier 인계, 적재 계획, 배송 예외 처리의 오류로 이어질 수 있는데, 기준 문서가 이를 막는 공통 의미를 제공하지 못한다.
  - root hypothesis: 물류 수량 단위를 canonical concept로 승격하지 않고 source-system convention을 통합 기준 안에 그대로 허용한 설계 결정.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:37-38`, `materialized-input.md:48-49`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33-49; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-002`, `finding-002.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:37`, `finding-002.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:38`, `finding-002.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:48`, `finding-002.cause-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준은 주문·화물·배송 값이 운임 산정, 적재 계획, 라벨, carrier handoff, 배송 예외 처리에서 일관되게 해석되도록 해야 한다. 그런데 Sku.weight와 Sku.dims가 kg/lb 또는 문자열 단위를 소스 시스템 관례에 맡기고 Shipment.total_weight가 OrderLine 중량 합으로 저장되면, 서로 다른 단위 값이 합산·전달될 수 있다. 이는 통합 기준이 단위 불일치 위험을 예방하지 못하고 각 연동의 관례 차이를 운영 경로로 전파하게 만든다.
  - root cause: 문제의 시작점은 물류 수량 단위를 canonical concept로 승격하지 않고 source-system convention을 통합 모델 안에서 허용한 결정이다. upstream deliberation은 이를 더 넓은 measurement-continuity root로 좁혔으며, 측정값에는 canonical unit, 원천 단위, 변환 기준, 계산 timestamp/version, 단위 미확정 값의 계산 제외 규칙이 함께 있어야 한다고 정리했다.
  - causal path: causal path는 Sku.weight가 단위 없는 number로 남고, Sku.dims가 소스별 단위 문자열로 남으며, Shipment.total_weight가 구성 OrderLine 중량 합으로 저장되는 흐름이다. 이 조합은 단위가 다른 원천 값이 동일한 계산·배송 연동 값으로 들어갈 가능성을 만들고, 결과적으로 사용자가 요청한 단위 정합성과 물류 운영 위험 검토 기준을 약화한다.
  - action: weight와 dims를 수치 값과 canonical unit으로 분리하고 원천 단위와 변환 시점·방법을 보존해야 한다. Shipment.total_weight는 canonical unit 기반 계산 결과와 계산 기준 시점/version을 포함해야 하며, 단위가 확정되지 않은 값은 출하 확정 계산에 사용할 수 없도록 모델링해야 한다. remediation은 issue-005/issue-011의 measurement-unit cluster와 함께 처리해 중복 수정 없이 같은 root를 닫는 순서가 적절하다.

- issue-003 (medium): State interpretation is materially weak because order fulfillment, shipment, carrier tracking, and displayed ETA/state values are not governed by a canonical versioned status model; instead, meaning and authority are delegated to independent systems, integrations, and operations judgment.

Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 즉 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서로서 상태 모델과 권위를 검토해야 한다는 기준이다. 대상은 상태 전이를 통합 의미로 정렬하지 않고 연동별 해석에 위임해, 동일 배송 사건이 주문 상태·화물 상태·추적 이벤트·ETA 표시에서 서로 다른 의미를 가질 수 있게 한다. Source finding context: 캐리어 이벤트, TMS shipment 상태, OMS 주문 상태, 운영팀 ETA 수정이 서로 다른 시점이나 의미를 가지며 통합 화면·후속 프로세스에서 함께 사용될 때. Source finding context: 상태와 ETA의 canonical authority 및 precedence를 온톨로지에서 정의하지 않고 각 시스템·연동·운영 판단에 분산시킨 설계 결정. Source finding context: Order.fulfillment_status는 OMS가 관리하고 Shipment.status는 별도 TMS 성격의 화물 상태로 정의된다. Source finding context: 무결성 규칙은 Order, Shipment, TrackingEvent 상태를 각 시스템이 독립 관리한다고 명시한다. Source finding context: ETA는 carrier API와 운영팀 수기 조정값이 섞이고 최종 표시값이 운영팀 판단에 따른다고 되어 있어 표시 권위와 원천 권위가 분리되지 않는다. Source finding context: 이 조합은 사용자가 요청한 상태 모델·권위 정합성 기준에 대해 통합 기준 문서의 목적 부합성을 낮춘다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type와 Shipment.status 상태 확장 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:44, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:63, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:65, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:66, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:113 Source finding context: 새 운송사 상태 코드나 외부 표준 변경을 수용하려면 enum과 연동별 매핑을 반복 수정해야 하며 상태 의미의 연속성이 보장되지 않는다. Source finding context: 변경 내성의 핵심은 외부 표준이 바뀌어도 기존 이벤트와 규칙의 의미를 보존하는 것이다. 현재 모델은 carrier-native 상태, canonical 상태, 매핑 버전, 적용 기간을 분리하지 않아 상태 변화가 발생하면 enum 확장과 개별 연동 로직 수정이 동시에 필요하다. 그 결과 과거 TrackingEvent가 당시 어떤 매핑 규칙으로 Shipment.status에 영향을 줬는지 재현하기 어렵고, WMS/TMS/ERP 간 상태 통합 규칙도 연동별 구현으로 흩어진다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent에는 carrier_code, native_status_code, native_status_version을 보존하고, 별도 StatusMapping 개념으로 canonical Shipment.status, mapping_version, effective_from/to, precedence를 모델링한다. enum은 내부 canonical 상태로 한정하고 외부 코드는 데이터로 확장 가능하게 둔다.   - impact: Past and current states may be interpreted by different integration rules, weakening reproducibility and causing drift in delivered, delayed, exception, or completed decisions.   - root hypothesis: Carrier-native statuses, canonical shipment/order statuses, mapping precedence, and mapping versions are not modeled as first-class concepts.

.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:44`, `materialized-input.md:46`, `materialized-input.md:63`, `materialized-input.md:65`, `materialized-input.md:66`, `materialized-input.md:113`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:44, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:63, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:65, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:66, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:113`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-003`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-011`, `rel-010`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-010`, `finding-003.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:44-47`, `finding-003.cause-002`, `finding-003.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:111-113`, `finding-003.cause-004`, `materialized-input.md:49`, `materialized-input.md:117`, `finding-003.cause-005`, `finding-011.cause-001`, `finding-011.cause-002`, `finding-011.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-005`, `rel-011`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-006`, `rel-012`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline purpose because the ontology is supposed to provide a common state and authority reference for order, shipment, tracking, and carrier coordination. When carrier events, TMS shipment statuses, OMS order statuses, ETA overrides, or new carrier status codes are used together, delivered, delayed, exception, and completed decisions can drift by integration or time period rather than following one reproducible baseline.
  - root cause: The starting point is that carrier-native statuses, canonical shipment/order statuses, mapping precedence, and mapping versions are not first-class modeled concepts. Without those concepts, the ontology has no stable authority for preserving external status inputs, converting them into internal meanings, deciding conflicts or freshness, or reconstructing which mapping rule applied historically.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported by two converging findings: axiology shows that Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type, and ETA display authority are independently managed or left to integrations and operations judgment; evolution shows that fixed enums and integration-specific mappings cannot preserve meaning across new carriers, changed status codes, or historical replay. Together, those steps explain why the same shipping event can produce inconsistent current or historical meanings across OMS, TMS, carrier tracking, ERP-facing processes, and integrated screens.
  - action: Add a canonical fulfillment/shipment state model and separate it from native carrier status data. Preserve carrier_code, native_status_code, and native_status_version; introduce versioned StatusMapping with canonical status, mapping_version, effective dates, precedence, conflict resolution, freshness rules, and displayed-state authority. ETA overrides should be modeled separately from carrier ETA with audit and display-decision rules, so source truth, operational override, and final presentation do not collapse into one field.

- issue-005 (medium): SKU weight, SKU dimensions, and Shipment.total_weight need canonical measurement semantics because the current model leaves physical quantities as source-local numbers or strings that cannot be trusted across warehouse, carrier, or unit-rule changes.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6

  - accepted lenses: coverage, evolution
  - remaining disagreement: 0/2 deliberation participants
