- issue-003 (medium)
  - issue: State interpretation is materially weak because order fulfillment, shipment, carrier tracking, and displayed ETA/state values are not governed by a canonical versioned status model; instead, meaning and authority are delegated to independent systems, integrations, and operations judgment.
  - target: State interpretation is delegated to independent systems and integrations rather than a canonical versioned status model. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type, status integration rule Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:19-22,44-49,60-68,111-117; .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: 상태 모델의 매핑과 최종 표시 권위를 각 연동·운영 판단에 맡기는 결정은 통합 기준 문서의 상태·권위 정합 목적에서 drift를 만든다. Source finding context: 가치 판단의 authority anchor는 review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11의 사용자 의도, 즉 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서로서 상태 모델과 권위를 검토해야 한다는 기준이다. 대상은 상태 전이를 통합 의미로 정렬하지 않고 연동별 해석에 위임해, 동일 배송 사건이 주문 상태·화물 상태·추적 이벤트·ETA 표시에서 서로 다른 의미를 가질 수 있게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 상태 모델과 권위 정합성을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 캐리어 이벤트, TMS shipment 상태, OMS 주문 상태, 운영팀 ETA 수정이 서로 다른 시점이나 의미를 가지며 통합 화면·후속 프로세스에서 함께 사용될 때. Source finding context: 상태와 ETA의 canonical authority 및 precedence를 온톨로지에서 정의하지 않고 각 시스템·연동·운영 판단에 분산시킨 설계 결정. Source finding context: 무결성 규칙은 Order, Shipment, TrackingEvent 상태를 각 시스템이 독립 관리한다고 명시한다. Source finding context: ETA는 carrier API와 운영팀 수기 조정값이 섞이고 최종 표시값이 운영팀 판단에 따른다고 되어 있어 표시 권위와 원천 권위가 분리되지 않는다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type와 Shipment.status 상태 확장 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:44, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:63, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:65, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:66, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:113 Source finding context: 새 운송사 상태 코드나 외부 표준 변경을 수용하려면 enum과 연동별 매핑을 반복 수정해야 하며 상태 의미의 연속성이 보장되지 않는다. Source finding context: 변경 내성의 핵심은 외부 표준이 바뀌어도 기존 이벤트와 규칙의 의미를 보존하는 것이다. 현재 모델은 carrier-native 상태, canonical 상태, 매핑 버전, 적용 기간을 분리하지 않아 상태 변화가 발생하면 enum 확장과 개별 연동 로직 수정이 동시에 필요하다. 그 결과 과거 TrackingEvent가 당시 어떤 매핑 규칙으로 Shipment.status에 영향을 줬는지 재현하기 어렵고, WMS/TMS/ERP 간 상태 통합 규칙도 연동별 구현으로 흩어진다.   - failure condition: Carrier events, TMS shipment statuses, OMS order statuses, external status-code changes, or operations ETA/state decisions are used together in integrated screens or downstream processes. Source finding context: 캐리어 이벤트, TMS shipment 상태, OMS 주문 상태, 운영팀 ETA 수정이 서로 다른 시점이나 의미를 가지며 통합 화면·후속 프로세스에서 함께 사용될 때.   - rationale: Add a canonical fulfillment/shipment state model and separate it from native carrier status data. Preserve carrier_code, native_status_code, and native_status_version; introduce versioned StatusMapping with canonical status, mapping_version, effective dates, precedence, conflict resolution, freshness rules, and displayed-state authority. ETA overrides should be modeled separately from carrier ETA with audit and display-decision rules, so source truth, operational override, and final presentation do not collapse into one field.

Preserve carrier_code, native_status_code, and native_status_version; introduce versioned StatusMapping with canonical status, mapping_version, effective dates, precedence, conflict resolution, freshness rules, and displayed-state authority. ETA overrides should be modeled separately from carrier ETA with audit and display-decision rules, so source truth, operational override, and final presentation do not collapse into one field.

- issue-005 (medium)
  - issue: SKU weight, SKU dimensions, and Shipment.total_weight need canonical measurement semantics because the current model leaves physical quantities as source-local numbers or strings that cannot be trusted across warehouse, carrier, or unit-rule changes.
  - target: SKU and shipment measurement quantities lack canonical unit semantics and are brittle under warehouse or carrier unit changes. Source finding context: Quantity and measurement unit coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:28-33, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:35-44 Source finding context: SKU dimensions and weights lack canonical units, while shipment weight is derived from them. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration depends on consistent physical quantities for rating, routing, capacity, labels, and customs-adjacent operations even if international shipment is out of scope. Deriving shipment weight from unitless or source-local measurements creates a coverage gap in the unit dimension of the ontology. Source finding context: Add measurement value objects or attributes for numeric value plus canonical unit, dimensional components, conversion basis, and source unit; require derived shipment totals to specify unit and derivation timestamp. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for SKU, freight, and carrier integration. Source finding context: When different warehouses or systems provide kg/lb or dimension strings with different conventions, shipment totals and carrier-facing measurements cannot be interpreted reliably from the ontology. Source finding context: The model can permit silent unit mixing in operational integration points such as cartonization, carrier rating, routing, and freight reconciliation. Source finding context: Measurement units are not modeled as first-class concepts or required attributes for physical quantities. Source finding context: SKU weight is a number whose unit follows each warehouse's local convention. Source finding context: SKU dimensions are a free-form string whose units vary by source system. Source finding context: Shipment total weight is calculated and stored from order-line weights, inheriting the missing-unit problem. Source finding context: Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight 단위 모델 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:37, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:38, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48 Source finding context: 창고·운송사별 단위 확장 시 기존 중량/치수 값의 의미가 변환 불가능해져 출하 중량 데이터의 연속성이 끊긴다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 단위는 나중에 붙일 표시 정보가 아니라 수량의 의미를 구성하는 권위 정보다. 현재 구조는 단위가 값과 분리되어 있지 않아, lb 창고와 kg 창고를 동시에 운영하거나 운송사가 부피중량/실중량 규칙을 요구하는 변경이 오면 기존 숫자들을 해석·변환·검증할 기준이 없다. 특히 total_weight가 출하 확정 시 저장되는 파생값이므로, 단위 보존 없이 계산된 과거 shipment 데이터는 단위 표준화 이후에도 신뢰 가능한 비교 기준으로 유지되기 어렵다. Source finding context: 중량과 치수를 Quantity 값 객체로 모델링해 value, unit, measurement_system/source, measured_at 또는 calculation_basis를 포함한다. Shipment.total_weight도 구성 SKU 중량의 단순 숫자 합이 아니라 단위 변환 규칙과 계산 버전을 가진 파생 측정값으로 정의한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합에서 SKU, 출하, 운송 요율·제약 판단에 쓰이는 수량 단위의 공통 기준 제공. Source finding context: kg/lb가 혼재된 창고, 다른 치수 단위를 쓰는 소스 시스템, 또는 단위 민감 운송사/요율 규칙을 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 기존 숫자 필드의 단위 의미가 보존되지 않아 신규 단위 체계와 기존 출하 데이터를 같은 기준으로 변환하거나 감사하기 어렵다. Source finding context: 측정값이 단위와 계산 기준을 포함한 독립 개념이 아니라 number/string 필드에 소스 관례로 저장된다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 숫자이며 창고별 계량 관례인 kg 또는 lb를 그대로 쓴다고 정의된다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이고 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다고 정의된다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 저장되지만 단위 또는 계산 기준 필드가 없다.
  - failure condition: Different warehouses, source systems, carriers, or rate rules introduce kg/lb or dimension-convention differences while Shipment.total_weight is calculated from unitless SKU values. Source finding context: When different warehouses or systems provide kg/lb or dimension strings with different conventions, shipment totals and carrier-facing measurements cannot be interpreted reliably from the ontology. Source finding context: kg/lb가 혼재된 창고, 다른 치수 단위를 쓰는 소스 시스템, 또는 단위 민감 운송사/요율 규칙을 추가하는 경우.
  - candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Model weight and dimensions as quantity value objects containing value, canonical unit, source or measurement system, measured/calculated timestamp, conversion basis, and source unit where relevant. Derived shipment totals should also be modeled as derived measurement values with unit, derivation timestamp, conversion rule, and calculation version, so unit normalization is established before shipment, carrier-rating, routing, capacity, and audit workflows depend on the values.
  - remediation: Model weight and dimensions as quantity value objects containing value, canonical unit, source or measurement system, measured/calculated timestamp, conversion basis, and source unit where relevant. Derived shipment totals should also be modeled as derived measurement values with unit, derivation timestamp, conversion rule, and calculation version, so unit normalization is established before shipment, carrier-rating, routing, capacity, and audit workflows depend on the values.
  - verification: verify the remediation against the failure condition with a focused check before closing
- issue-006 (medium)
  - issue: Manual ETA overrides and inventory reconciliation changes are a material auditability gap: the ontology can hold changed ETA or inventory values, but it cannot represent who changed them, when, on what basis, or what prior and new values were involved.
  - target: Manual ETA overrides and inventory reconciliation changes lack audit evidence for actor, time, basis, and prior/new values. Source finding context: Manual and reconciliation audit evidence coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:44, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:76-79, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:110-112 Source finding context: Manual ETA overrides and inventory reconciliation adjustments lack audit evidence concepts. Source finding context: The coverage lens asks whether controlled actions have evidence for actor, time, and basis. Manual ETA changes and inventory reconciliation directly affect customer communication and stock truth, so a concept baseline that omits audit evidence cannot support accountability or dispute analysis. Source finding context: Add audit/control entities such as `ManualOverride`, `InventoryAdjustment`, or `OperationalDecision` with actor, occurred_at, source value, prior value, new value, reason, approval/reference evidence, and affected entity references. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: Use as an operationally trustworthy WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline. Source finding context: When an ETA or inventory value changes due to manual judgment or batch reconciliation, the ontology cannot represent who changed it, when, from what evidence, or why. Source finding context: Operational risk increases because customer-facing and accounting/warehouse reconciliation changes cannot be audited or challenged from the shared model. Source finding context: The ontology represents final mutable values but omits control-action evidence concepts for manual and batch changes. Source finding context: Shipment ETA can be adjusted manually by the field operations team. Source finding context: The final displayed ETA mixes carrier API values with manual operation-team judgment. Source finding context: Inventory quantities are held separately by WMS and ERP and adjusted through nightly reconciliation, but no adjustment evidence concept is modeled.
  - failure condition: ETA or inventory values change due to manual operations judgment or nightly reconciliation. Source finding context: When an ETA or inventory value changes due to manual judgment or batch reconciliation, the ontology cannot represent who changed it, when, from what evidence, or why.
  - candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Add audit/control concepts for manual overrides, operational decisions, and inventory adjustments before the next stage. These concepts should link each controlled change to actor, occurred_at, source or basis value, prior value, new value, reason, approval or reference evidence, and affected shipment or inventory entities, so ETA and inventory-truth changes become traceable rather than only final-state values.
  - remediation: Add audit/control concepts for manual overrides, operational decisions, and inventory adjustments before the next stage. These concepts should link each controlled change to actor, occurred_at, source or basis value, prior value, new value, reason, approval or reference evidence, and affected shipment or inventory entities, so ETA and inventory-truth changes become traceable rather than only final-state values.
  - verification: verify the remediation against the failure condition with a focused check before closing
- issue-007 (medium)
  - issue: Common non-happy-path and post-delivery fulfillment outcomes cannot currently be represented as order or shipment lifecycle states. The lifecycle model should be fixed before the next stage because it otherwise forces materially different outcomes into happy-path states or external handling.
  - target: Common non-happy-path and post-delivery outcomes cannot be represented as order or shipment lifecycle states. Source finding context: Order and shipment lifecycle coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:14-17, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:39-42, logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:55-63 Source finding context: The lifecycle states cover only the happy path and omit cancellation, failure, return, correction, and post-delivery exception handling. Source finding context: Fulfillment operations require non-happy-path and post-terminal coverage: cancellations, failed delivery, loss/damage, return-to-sender, returns, redelivery, proof-of-delivery correction, and reopened exceptions. Without those categories, the concept model cannot express common operational outcomes or distinguish final completion from later correction. Source finding context: Extend lifecycle models with explicit non-happy-path and post-delivery states/events, such as canceled, delivery_failed, exception_open, exception_resolved, return_initiated, returned, corrected/reissued, and define how tracking events transition or annotate shipment/order state. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-005 Source finding context: Use as a concept baseline for order, shipment, and delivery state models. Source finding context: When an order or shipment is canceled, fails delivery, returns, receives a carrier exception, or needs post-delivery correction, the ontology has no lifecycle category to represent the outcome without overloading `delivered` or external handling. Source finding context: Integrations can diverge on state interpretation and cannot consistently model operational risk events that materially affect customer service, inventory, and settlement. Source finding context: Lifecycle coverage is limited to forward fulfillment states and does not include exception resolution or terminal correction concepts. Source finding context: Order fulfillment status progresses from received to delivered with no cancellation, return, failure, or correction states. Source finding context: Shipment status progresses from created to delivered and notes that delivered is final. Source finding context: Tracking events can report `exception`, but no shipment/order lifecycle state or resolution concept corresponds to that event.
  - failure condition: An order or shipment is canceled, fails delivery, returns, receives a carrier exception, or requires post-delivery correction. Source finding context: When an order or shipment is canceled, fails delivery, returns, receives a carrier exception, or needs post-delivery correction, the ontology has no lifecycle category to represent the outcome without overloading `delivered` or external handling.
  - candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Extend the lifecycle models with explicit non-happy-path and post-delivery states or events, such as canceled, delivery_failed, exception_open, exception_resolved, return_initiated, returned, corrected, or reissued. Also define how tracking events transition or annotate shipment and order state, because the tracking exception concept must connect to the canonical lifecycle rather than remain an isolated event value.
  - remediation: Extend the lifecycle models with explicit non-happy-path and post-delivery states or events, such as canceled, delivery_failed, exception_open, exception_resolved, return_initiated, returned, corrected, or reissued. Also define how tracking events transition or annotate shipment and order state, because the tracking exception concept must connect to the canonical lifecycle rather than remain an isolated event value.
  - verification: verify the remediation against the failure condition with a focused check before closing
- issue-008 (medium)
  - issue: DeliveryLeg should not remain a subtype of Shipment. The current model makes a route segment inherit the meaning of an end-to-end warehouse-to-customer shipment, so leg-level records are classified and related as whole shipments even when they only represent one segment of a route.
  - target: DeliveryLeg inherits Shipment meaning even though route segments and end-to-end shipments have different classification, lifecycle, and fulfillment scopes. Source finding context: Shipment와 DeliveryLeg 상속 구조 Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:41, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:48, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:52, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:54, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:105, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:106, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:107, .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:108 Source finding context: 다구간·환적·역물류 확장 시 배송 구간이 전체 화물의 속성과 주문 충족 관계를 상속해 경로 모델을 수정해야 한다. Source finding context: 변경 내성 관점에서 전체 Shipment와 구간 DeliveryLeg는 lifecycle, 상태, 운송사, ETA, 주문 충족 책임이 다르게 진화하는 축이다. 현재는 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment를 상속하므로 구간이 전체 shipment의 fulfills 관계와 total_weight 의미에 끌려 들어간다. 다구간 TMS 통합이 들어오면 구간별 carrier/status/ETA는 필요하지만 구간이 주문 전체를 충족한다고 볼 수 없는 경우가 생겨 기존 구조가 확장보다 재분류를 요구한다. Source finding context: Shipment를 전체 consignment/fulfillment movement로 두고 DeliveryLeg는 상속이 아니라 route component 관계로 연결한다. ShipmentRoute 또는 LegAssignment를 추가해 leg_seq, from/to, carrier, leg_status, leg_eta를 구간 속성으로 분리하고, fulfills는 전체 Shipment 또는 별도 FulfillmentAllocation에만 두도록 범위를 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004 Source finding context: TMS 통합에서 전체 화물, 배송 구간, 주문 충족 관계를 확장 가능한 개념 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 복수 구간, 복수 운송사, 허브 환적, 구간별 상태/ETA 또는 반품 경로를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: 구간 엔티티가 전체 Shipment 의미를 상속해 신규 경로 유형을 기존 구조 수정 없이 추가하기 어렵고, 주문 충족 관계가 구간 단위로 잘못 확장될 위험이 있다. Source finding context: 전체 화물 이동과 경로 구간이 상속 관계로 결합되어 각자의 독립적인 lifecycle과 관계 범위가 분리되지 않는다. Source finding context: Shipment는 창고에서 고객으로 가는 물리적 화물 이동으로 정의되고 DeliveryLeg도 Shipment의 일종이라고 설명된다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 is_a: Shipment로 선언되어 전체 Shipment의 status, total_weight, eta, carrier_ref 구조를 상속한다. Source finding context: fulfills 관계는 Shipment에서 Order로 정의되어 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment 하위형인 한 구간에도 주문 충족 관계가 적용될 수 있다. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment hierarchy Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:35-49 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is declared as a Shipment even though its own definition allows segments that do not satisfy the Shipment definition. Source finding context: The conflict is inter-claim: the superclass definition at lines 35-36 imposes a warehouse-to-customer movement shape, and the subclass definition at lines 47-49 allows partial route segments. This makes at least the example leg instances unsatisfiable as Shipment instances without weakening either the Shipment definition or the subtype assertion. For a WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline, this also propagates Shipment-level status, carrier, and fulfillment relations onto leg-level objects whose lifecycle is not necessarily equivalent to an end-to-end shipment. Source finding context: Separate end-to-end `Shipment` from `DeliveryLeg` composition, for example model `Shipment` has many `DeliveryLeg`s instead of `DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment`; or broaden `Shipment` to a generic transport movement and introduce a distinct end-to-end shipment concept for warehouse-to-customer fulfillment. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across orders, cargo, and delivery. Source finding context: When a delivery route contains warehouse-to-hub or hub-to-customer legs, those DeliveryLeg instances must both be Shipment instances and not satisfy the stated Shipment definition. Source finding context: The ontology cannot consistently classify leg-level and shipment-level records, weakening integration rules that rely on inherited Shipment status, carrier, and fulfillment semantics. Source finding context: The model uses subtype inheritance for a part-of route segment relationship. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is declared with `is_a: Shipment`. Source finding context: `Shipment` is defined as an end-to-end warehouse-to-customer movement. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is defined as a route segment and explicitly includes warehouse-to-hub and hub-to-customer examples, which are partial movements rather than each being warehouse-to-customer movements.
  - failure condition: Multi-leg, hub-transfer, multiple-carrier, leg-specific ETA/status, or reverse-logistics routes require DeliveryLeg instances that are not themselves warehouse-to-customer shipments. Source finding context: 복수 구간, 복수 운송사, 허브 환적, 구간별 상태/ETA 또는 반품 경로를 추가하는 경우. Source finding context: When a delivery route contains warehouse-to-hub or hub-to-customer legs, those DeliveryLeg instances must both be Shipment instances and not satisfy the stated Shipment definition.

  - rationale: Separate end-to-end Shipment from DeliveryLeg composition. Model Shipment as owning or being connected to ordered route legs through a ShipmentRoute or LegAssignment structure, with leg_seq, from/to, carrier, leg_status, and leg_eta on the leg or assignment. Keep fulfills on the whole Shipment, or move allocation semantics to a separate FulfillmentAllocation if fulfillment must be split explicitly.
  - remediation: Separate end-to-end Shipment from DeliveryLeg composition. Model Shipment as owning or being connected to ordered route legs through a ShipmentRoute or LegAssignment structure, with leg_seq, from/to, carrier, leg_status, and leg_eta on the leg or assignment. Keep fulfills on the whole Shipment, or move allocation semantics to a separate FulfillmentAllocation if fulfillment must be split explicitly.

- issue-009 (medium)
  - issue: DeliveryLeg must not remain modeled as a subtype of Shipment. In the reviewed ontology, that inheritance makes a route segment look like the same kind of thing as the whole shipment, so leg-level TMS state can be mistaken for whole-shipment fulfillment state.
  - target: DeliveryLeg as a Shipment makes leg-level TMS states and whole-shipment fulfillment semantics appear to be the same concept. WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서에서 이 상속은 출하 단위 추적, 구간 상태, 주문 충족 여부의 의미를 섞어 시스템 간 매핑 오류를 만들 수 있다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 하위유형이 아니라 ShipmentRoute 또는 ShipmentLeg 같은 구성 요소로 모델링하고, Shipment가 여러 DeliveryLeg를 갖는 관계를 둔다. 구간 상태와 전체 Shipment 상태를 별도 enum으로 분리하고, fulfills 관계는 전체 Shipment 또는 실제 주문라인 충족 단위에만 붙인다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 경로의 한 구간으로 정의되어 전체 창고-고객 이동인 Shipment와 다른 의미를 가진다. Source finding context: 상속 때문에 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment의 속성과 fulfills(Order) 관계까지 받는 완전한 Shipment처럼 해석된다.

  - target: Weight and dimension fields can mean kg, lb, or source-specific dimension systems under the same names. Source finding context: Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33,37,38,48 Source finding context: 중량·치수 속성이 단위를 의미의 일부로 고정하지 않아 같은 이름이 kg/lb와 소스별 치수 체계를 동시에 뜻한다. Source finding context: weight와 dims는 수치만으로 의미가 완성되지 않고 단위가 포함되어야 한다. 단위 없는 'weight'와 문자열 'dims'는 시스템별로 다른 의미를 같은 속성명 아래에 숨기며, total_weight 같은 파생값은 서로 다른 단위가 합산될 위험이 있다. Source finding context: weight_value와 weight_uom, dimension_value 구조(length/width/height)와 dimension_uom을 분리하거나 표준 단위로 정규화한다. total_weight에는 계산 기준 단위와 계산 시점, 원천 SKU/OrderLine 단위 변환 규칙을 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 단위 정합성을 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 여러 창고나 소스 시스템의 SKU가 한 Shipment에 포함되거나 total_weight가 운임/적재/배송 제한 판단에 사용될 때. Source finding context: 동일 숫자가 kg 또는 lb로 해석될 수 있어 중량 합계와 치수 기반 운영 판단이 의미적으로 신뢰 불가능해진다. Source finding context: 측정값 속성의 의미를 구성하는 단위를 별도 권위 있는 속성이나 표준 단위로 모델링하지 않았다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 kg 또는 lb를 창고별 관례대로 사용한다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 문자열이며 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다르다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 원천 중량들의 합으로 저장되어 단위 혼합이 파생값 의미까지 전파된다.
  - failure condition: Multiple warehouses or source systems contribute SKU measurements to one Shipment, or total_weight is used for freight, loading, or delivery restrictions. Source finding context: 여러 창고나 소스 시스템의 SKU가 한 Shipment에 포함되거나 total_weight가 운임/적재/배송 제한 판단에 사용될 때.
  - candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: Consolidate the fix under issue-005's canonical measurement quantity work: represent weight and dimensions as explicit value-plus-unit fields or normalized quantities, and make total_weight carry its calculation unit, calculation time, conversion rules, and conversion history. This should be addressed before relying on shipment totals for freight, loading, or delivery constraints because the derived value depends on correct source-unit interpretation.
  - remediation: Consolidate the fix under issue-005's canonical measurement quantity work: represent weight and dimensions as explicit value-plus-unit fields or normalized quantities, and make total_weight carry its calculation unit, calculation time, conversion rules, and conversion history. This should be addressed before relying on shipment totals for freight, loading, or delivery constraints because the derived value depends on correct source-unit interpretation.
  - verification: verify the remediation against the failure condition with a focused check before closing
- issue-012 (medium)
  - issue: InventoryAggregate.available_qty should be carried forward as a material issue because it is named and consumed like current available inventory while representing a nightly snapshot used for intraday allocation.
  - target: The available_qty name and use obscure whether allocation reads current availability or a stale snapshot. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86,90,112 Source finding context: available_qty라는 이름이 현재 가용 재고를 뜻하지만 실제로는 야간 스냅샷 값이다. Source finding context: 'available'은 주문 시점의 할당 가능 수량처럼 읽히지만, 모델의 설명은 전일 또는 야간 배치 시점의 스냅샷에 가깝다. 이름과 시간성이 어긋나면 OMS/WMS/ERP가 같은 값을 현재 가용 재고로 오해할 수 있다. Source finding context: available_qty_snapshot, snapshot_as_of 같은 시간성 있는 이름과 속성으로 바꾸거나, 현재 할당 가능 수량은 실시간 계산 개념으로 분리한다. 일중 할당 규칙은 어떤 시점의 재고를 권위로 삼는지 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004 Source finding context: 주문·재고 통합에서 재고 가용성의 의미와 시간 기준을 명확히 하는 목적. Source finding context: 일중 주문 할당이 nightly snapshot available_qty를 현재 가용량으로 사용할 때. Source finding context: 재고 할당 기준이 현재 상태인지 스냅샷인지 불분명해 과할당 또는 보수적 미할당 같은 운영 위험을 낳는다. Source finding context: 시간성 있는 스냅샷 값을 현재성을 암시하는 available_qty 이름으로 모델링했다. Source finding context: available_qty는 매일 밤 배치로 저장되는 스냅샷으로 설명된다. Source finding context: 동시에 일중 주문 할당도 이 값을 읽는다고 규칙화되어 현재 가용량처럼 사용된다.
  - failure condition: Intraday order allocation uses nightly snapshot available_qty as if it were current available stock. Source finding context: 일중 주문 할당이 nightly snapshot available_qty를 현재 가용량으로 사용할 때.
  - candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: Rename and model the value as a timestamped snapshot, such as available_qty_snapshot with snapshot_as_of and source traceability, or split current allocatable quantity into a separate real-time derived concept with explicit authority and freshness. This should be coordinated with issue-001's broader inventory temporality work so the available_qty-specific ambiguity is fixed without duplicating generic inventory authority remediation.
  - remediation: Rename and model the value as a timestamped snapshot, such as available_qty_snapshot with snapshot_as_of and source traceability, or split current allocatable quantity into a separate real-time derived concept with explicit authority and freshness. This should be coordinated with issue-001's broader inventory temporality work so the available_qty-specific ambiguity is fixed without duplicating generic inventory authority remediation.
  - verification: verify the remediation against the failure condition with a focused check before closing
- issue-015 (medium)
  - issue: Issue-015 is a material route-composition gap: the ontology cannot reconstruct which DeliveryLeg records compose an end-to-end Shipment, or their order, for multi-leg delivery paths.
  - target: The ontology cannot reconstruct the route legs belonging to a shipment. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Shipment-DeliveryLeg route structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40-58,105-109 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is typed as a Shipment but no structure connects an end-to-end Shipment to its legs. Source finding context: An is_a link says a DeliveryLeg is a kind of Shipment; it does not connect a multi-leg shipment to the ordered sequence of legs that compose its route. For a delivery concept model, this leaves the physical delivery path disconnected from the shipment it is supposed to detail. Source finding context: Introduce an explicit composition/sequence relation, for example Shipment has_many DeliveryLeg ordered by leg_seq, or a Route entity containing DeliveryLeg instances, and define whether TrackingEvent attaches to the whole Shipment, a leg, or both. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002 Source finding context: Using the ontology as a concept baseline for shipment and delivery integration. Source finding context: When a shipment has multiple delivery legs, the model cannot reconstruct which legs belong to the shipment or the ordered route for that shipment. Source finding context: TMS/carrier integrations cannot reliably connect route segments, locations, and tracking events back to the end-to-end shipment path using the ontology structure. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is modeled only through inheritance from Shipment, without a composition relation from a shipment or route to its legs. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg has leg_seq, from_ref, and to_ref, indicating a route segment, but no parent shipment or route reference. Source finding context: The only structural connection involving Shipment in the relations section is fulfills from Shipment to Order. Source finding context: TrackingEvent points to Shipment generally, so without a shipment-leg composition path events cannot be structurally localized to a route segment when needed.
  - failure condition: A shipment has multiple delivery legs or tracking events that must be localized to a route segment. Source finding context: When a shipment has multiple delivery legs, the model cannot reconstruct which legs belong to the shipment or the ordered route for that shipment.
  - candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Fix this before the next stage by adding an explicit ordered composition structure: either Shipment has_many DeliveryLeg ordered by leg_seq, or a Route entity contains ordered DeliveryLeg instances. Coordinate the fix with issue-008 because both share the inheritance-versus-composition cause, but preserve issue-015 as the distinct requirement for ordered route reconstruction and leg-level traceability. Also define whether TrackingEvent attaches to Shipment, DeliveryLeg, or both.
  - remediation: Fix this before the next stage by adding an explicit ordered composition structure: either Shipment has_many DeliveryLeg ordered by leg_seq, or a Route entity contains ordered DeliveryLeg instances. Coordinate the fix with issue-008 because both share the inheritance-versus-composition cause, but preserve issue-015 as the distinct requirement for ordered route reconstruction and leg-level traceability. Also define whether TrackingEvent attaches to Shipment, DeliveryLeg, or both.
  - verification: verify the remediation against the failure condition with a focused check before closing
- issue-016 (medium)
  - issue: InventoryAggregate is a material structural traceability issue: allocation reads available_qty from an aggregate SKU quantity, but the ontology does not show which warehouse-scoped InventoryRecord records, source authorities, snapshot/as-of metadata, or calculation basis produced that value.
  - target: Allocation reads an aggregate inventory number whose source records and derivation cannot be traced. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / InventoryAggregate source structure Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-90,111-116 Source finding context: InventoryAggregate is not structurally connected to the InventoryRecord records it aggregates. Source finding context: The ontology declares InventoryAggregate as the quantity used for allocation, including intraday orders, and notes that WMS and ERP each hold inventory truth that is reconciled by nightly batch. Without an explicit aggregate-from/source relation, the inventory path from warehouse-level records to enterprise availability is structurally incomplete. Source finding context: Add an explicit derivation or aggregation relation from InventoryAggregate to its source InventoryRecord set, including scope such as warehouse set, source system/authority, and snapshot/as_of metadata if the aggregate is batch-produced. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003 Source finding context: Using the ontology as the inventory concept baseline for WMS/ERP allocation and reconciliation. Source finding context: When allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty, the model cannot trace the aggregate back to warehouse-level inventory records or their source systems. Source finding context: Consumers can read the allocation quantity but cannot verify, reconcile, or route disputes through the ontology structure, weakening trust in cross-system inventory integration. Source finding context: The aggregate inventory concept is represented as a standalone SKU quantity without a structural derivation path from warehouse inventory records or inventory authorities. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate has only sku_ref and available_qty; it has no reference to InventoryRecord, Warehouse, WMS, ERP, or a source snapshot. Source finding context: InventoryRecord is the warehouse/SKU inventory record, but no relation connects it to InventoryAggregate. Source finding context: The allocation rule relies on InventoryAggregate.available_qty, making the disconnected aggregate a live structural dependency for order fulfillment.
  - failure condition: Allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty or trace it to warehouse-level inventory records and source systems. Source finding context: When allocation or reconciliation needs to explain available_qty, the model cannot trace the aggregate back to warehouse-level inventory records or their source systems.
  - candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: Add explicit derivation or aggregation relations from InventoryAggregate to the source InventoryRecord sets, including warehouse scope, source authority, snapshot/as-of metadata, and calculation basis. This should be carried forward as the narrow derivation and traceability facet linked to the broader inventory authority issue, so available_qty can be audited without duplicating the wider issue-001 concern.
  - remediation: Add explicit derivation or aggregation relations from InventoryAggregate to the source InventoryRecord sets, including warehouse scope, source authority, snapshot/as-of metadata, and calculation basis. This should be carried forward as the narrow derivation and traceability facet linked to the broader inventory authority issue, so available_qty can be audited without duplicating the wider issue-001 concern.
  - verification: verify the remediation against the failure condition with a focused check before closing

### Recommendations
- issue-013 (low): carrier ETA, operations-adjusted ETA, and displayed ETA may lose source meaning when represented as one eta value. Source finding context: Shipment.eta Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:49,117 Source finding context: eta가 캐리어 ETA와 운영팀 조정 ETA를 한 값으로 합쳐 예측값의 출처 의미를 잃는다. Source finding context: 캐리어가 제공한 ETA와 운영팀이 표시용으로 조정한 ETA는 출처와 사용 목적이 다르다. 단일 eta 속성은 원천 예측, 운영 보정, 최종 표시값을 같은 의미로 보이게 하므로 TMS/캐리어/고객 표시 간 의미 매핑이 흐려진다. Source finding context: carrier_eta, ops_adjusted_eta, displayed_eta를 분리하거나 eta 값에 source, authority, adjusted_by, adjusted_at을 명시한다. 최종 표시값이 운영팀 판단이라면 그 파생 규칙을 별도 속성 또는 규칙으로 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-9ee88c8b/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-005
